================ FILE: services/aiChatbot.js ================

// AI Chatbot Service — LOCAL ONLY (on-device llama.rn)
// Provides financial advice based on user's actual data

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import { formatCurrency } from '../utils/currencies';
import { filterTransactionsByPeriod, groupByCategory, calculateBudgetUsage } from '../utils/helpers';
import { getCategoryById, spendingCategories, incomeCategories } from '../utils/categories';
import { convertCurrency } from './exchangeRates';

export const popularQuestions = [
    { id: 1, text: 'How much did I spend this month?', icon: 'wallet-outline' },
    { id: 2, text: "What's my biggest spending category?", icon: 'pie-chart-outline' },
    { id: 3, text: 'Am I within my budget?', icon: 'flag-outline' },
    { id: 4, text: 'How much income did I earn this month?', icon: 'cash-outline' },
    { id: 5, text: "What's my net savings this month?", icon: 'business-outline' },
    { id: 6, text: 'Compare my spending this week vs last week', icon: 'stats-chart-outline' },
    { id: 7, text: 'Which days do I spend the most?', icon: 'calendar-outline' },
    { id: 8, text: 'How can I reduce my spending?', icon: 'cut-outline' },
    { id: 9, text: "What's my financial health summary?", icon: 'heart-outline' },
    { id: 10, text: 'Forecast my spending for the rest of the month', icon: 'sparkles-outline' },
];

function buildFinancialContext(state) {
    const { transactions, budgets, assets, vehicles = [], currency, currentDateKey, exchangeRates } = state;
    const referenceDate = currentDateKey ? new Date(currentDateKey) : new Date();
    const toBase = (amt, cur) => convertCurrency(amt || 0, cur || currency, currency, exchangeRates);

    const monthlySpending = filterTransactionsByPeriod(transactions, 'monthly', referenceDate).filter(t => t.type === 'spending');
    const monthlyIncome = filterTransactionsByPeriod(transactions, 'monthly', referenceDate).filter(t => t.type === 'income');
    const totalSpending = monthlySpending.reduce((s, t) => s + Math.abs(toBase(t.amount, t.currency)), 0);
    const totalIncome = monthlyIncome.reduce((s, t) => s + Math.abs(toBase(t.amount, t.currency)), 0);

    const weeklySpending = filterTransactionsByPeriod(transactions, 'weekly', referenceDate).filter(t => t.type === 'spending');
    const weeklyTotal = weeklySpending.reduce((s, t) => s + Math.abs(toBase(t.amount, t.currency)), 0);

    const dailySpending = filterTransactionsByPeriod(transactions, 'daily', referenceDate).filter(t => t.type === 'spending');
    const dailyTotal = dailySpending.reduce((s, t) => s + Math.abs(toBase(t.amount, t.currency)), 0);

    const catGroups = groupByCategory(monthlySpending, (t) => toBase(t.amount, t.currency));
    const categoryBreakdown = Object.entries(catGroups)
        .map(([id, data]) => ({
            name: getCategoryById(id).name,
            total: data.total,
            count: data.count,
        }))
        .sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total);

    const budgetStatus = budgets.map(b => {
        const usage = calculateBudgetUsage(transactions, b, referenceDate);
        return {
            category: b.category || 'all',
            period: b.period,
            limit: b.amount,
            spent: usage.spent,
            remaining: usage.remaining,
            percentage: usage.percentage.toFixed(1),
            isOver: usage.isOver,
        };
    });

    const assetInfo = assets.map(a => ({
        id: a.id,
        name: a.name,
        type: a.type,
        balance: a.balance,
    }));
    const totalAssets = assets.reduce((s, a) => s + toBase(a.balance, a.currency), 0);
    const recentTransactions = [...transactions]
        .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date).getTime() - new Date(a.date).getTime())
        .slice(0, 10);
    const topSpends = [...monthlySpending]
        .sort((a, b) => Math.abs(b.amount || 0) - Math.abs(a.amount || 0))
        .slice(0, 5);
    const recentByCategory = categoryBreakdown.slice(0, 6);

    return `## User's Financial Data (Currency: ${currency})
### This Month
- Total Income: ${formatCurrency(totalIncome, currency)}
- Total Spending: ${formatCurrency(totalSpending, currency)}
- Net Savings: ${formatCurrency(totalIncome - totalSpending, currency)}
- Transaction Count: ${monthlySpending.length} expenses, ${monthlyIncome.length} income entries

### This Week
- Weekly Spending: ${formatCurrency(weeklyTotal, currency)}

### Today
- Today's Spending: ${formatCurrency(dailyTotal, currency)}

### Top Spending Categories (This Month)
${categoryBreakdown.slice(0, 8).map((c, i) => `${i + 1}. ${c.name}: ${formatCurrency(c.total, currency)} (${c.count} transactions)`).join('\n')}

### Budget Status
${budgetStatus.length > 0 ? budgetStatus.map(b => `- ${b.category === 'all' ? 'All spending' : getCategoryById(b.category).name} ${b.period}: ${formatCurrency(b.spent, currency)} / ${formatCurrency(b.limit, currency)} (${b.percentage}%) ${b.isOver ? 'OVER BUDGET' : 'OK'}`).join('\n') : 'No budgets set'}

### Assets
${assetInfo.length > 0 ? assetInfo.map(a => `- ${a.name} (ID: ${a.id}, Type: ${a.type}): ${formatCurrency(a.balance, currency)}`).join('\n') : 'No assets tracked'}
Total Assets: ${formatCurrency(totalAssets, currency)}

### User Vehicles
${vehicles.length > 0 ? vehicles.map(v => `- ${v.name} (ID: ${v.id}, Type: ${v.type})`).join('\n') : 'No vehicles added'}

### Available Categories
- Spending: ${spendingCategories.map(c => `${c.name} (ID: ${c.id})`).join(', ')}
- Income: ${incomeCategories.map(c => `${c.name} (ID: ${c.id})`).join(', ')}

### Recent Transactions (Last 10)
${recentTransactions.map(t => {
        const sign = t.type === 'income' ? '+' : '-';
        return `- ${sign}${formatCurrency(Math.abs(t.amount), currency)} | ${getCategoryById(t.category).name} | ${t.description || 'No description'} | ${new Date(t.date).toLocaleDateString()}`;
    }).join('\n')}

### Top Spends This Month
${topSpends.length > 0 ? topSpends.map((t, i) => `${i + 1}. ${formatCurrency(Math.abs(t.amount), currency)} | ${getCategoryById(t.category).name} | ${t.description || 'No description'} | ${new Date(t.date).toLocaleDateString()}`).join('\n') : 'No spending this month'}

### Recent Category Pattern Summary
${recentByCategory.length > 0 ? recentByCategory.map(c => `- ${c.name}: ${formatCurrency(c.total, currency)} across ${c.count} transaction${c.count === 1 ? '' : 's'}`).join('\n') : 'No spending pattern yet'}`;
}

function estimateTokens(text) {
    return Math.ceil(String(text || '').length / 4);
}

function trimChatHistory(chatHistory = [], maxTokens = 1200) {
    const normalized = Array.isArray(chatHistory) ? chatHistory : [];
    const trimmed = [];
    let used = 0;
    for (let i = normalized.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const message = normalized[i];
        const cost = estimateTokens(message?.content) + 8;
        if (trimmed.length >= 24 || used + cost > maxTokens) break;
        trimmed.unshift(message);
        used += cost;
    }
    return trimmed;
}

function buildAttachmentContext(attachments = []) {
    const items = Array.isArray(attachments) ? attachments : [];
    if (items.length === 0) return '';

    return items.map((attachment, index) => {
        const name = attachment?.name || `Attachment ${index + 1}`;
        const mimeType = attachment?.mimeType || attachment?.type || 'unknown';
        const pageCount = Number.isFinite(attachment?.pageCount) ? `, ${attachment.pageCount} pages` : '';
        const text = String(attachment?.text || '').trim();
        const preview = text.length > 12000 ? `${text.slice(0, 12000)}\n\n[Attachment text truncated]` : text;

        return `- ${name} (${mimeType}${pageCount})\n${preview || '[No readable text extracted from attachment]'}`;
    }).join('\n\n');
}

const SYSTEM_PROMPT_GENERAL = `You are an AI Chatbot for FinanceFlow app, it is a financial tracking app, you must be a helpful personal finance assistant. You have access to the user's real financial data shown below. Use this data to give specific, actionable advice.

{CONTEXT}

Rules:
- Always reference the user's actual numbers when answering
- Be encouraging but honest about overspending
- Give specific, actionable advice
- Use the currency shown in the data
- Use bullet points for lists
- If asked about something not in the data, say so

CRITICAL INSTRUCTION: You MUST keep your responses extremely concise. You are STRICTLY LIMITED to a maximum of 3 short paragraphs. Do not over-explain.`;

const SYSTEM_PROMPT_TRANSACTION = `You are an AI Chatbot for FinanceFlow app. Your current task is to help the user record a transaction. 
You have access to the user's financial data below to know their available categories, assets, and vehicles.

{CONTEXT}

## Transaction JSON Schema
When the user describes a transaction they want to record, respond with a brief confirmation sentence followed by a JSON block in this exact format:

\`\`\`json
{
  "type": "spending" | "income",
  "amount": <number>,
  "category": "<categoryId>",
  "description": "<short description>",
  "date": "<ISO date string>",
  "assetId": "<assetId if mentioned>",
  "items": [{"name": "string", "quantity": 1, "price": <number>}],
  "vehicleId": "<vehicleId if transport, else null>",
  "vehicleLogType": "refuel" | "maintenance" | null,
  "vehicleOdometer": <number if mentioned, else null>,
  "vehicleVolume": <number (liters/kWh) if refuel mentioned, else null>
}
\`\`\`

### Category Selection Rules (IMPORTANT – follow exactly):
- Use category ID "transport" for ANY car/vehicle-related expense: fuel, petrol, diesel, refueling, EV charging, car repair, tire replacement, oil change, brake service, car wash, car service, vehicle maintenance, mechanic, workshop, etc.
- When category is "transport", you MUST also set:
  - "vehicleId" to the ID of the User Vehicle mentioned (if matched from "User Vehicles" section).
  - "vehicleVolume" to the liters/gallons/kWh if refuel is mentioned (e.g. "20 liters" -> 20).
  - "vehicleOdometer" to the odometer reading if mentioned (e.g. "105000km" -> 105000).
  - "vehicleLogType": "refuel" when the user mentions fuel, petrol, diesel, charging, fill up, litre, liter, gallon, kWh
  - "vehicleLogType": "maintenance" when the user mentions repair, service, tire, tyre, oil, brake, battery, mechanic, workshop, replaced, maintenance, tune-up, inspection, car wash
- Use "food" for meals, restaurants, groceries, delivery food
- Use "shopping" for general retail, clothing, electronics
- Use "health" for medical, pharmacy, doctor, hospital
- Use "entertainment" for movies, games, subscriptions, streaming
- Use "utilities" for electricity, water, internet, phone bills
- Use "education" for courses, books, tuition
- Use "travel" for flights, hotels, accommodation (non-car)
- Use "other" only when nothing else fits

### General Rules:
- "date": If the user says "yesterday", map it precisely to the date of yesterday.
- "assetId": If the user mentions paying with or receiving to an account (e.g., "BCA", "Wallet"), you MUST find the exact matching ID from the Assets list and set it here.
- "items": If the user bought specific items (e.g. "latte coffee"), include them in the "items" array.`;


// ─── LOCAL AI (on-device via llama.rn) ────────
export async function sendLocalChatMessage(userMessage, state, chatHistory = [], onToken, attachments = [], isTransactionMode = false) {
    const { chatCompletion, validateActiveModelForTask, getModelContextLength } = require('./localAI');
    validateActiveModelForTask('chatbot');

    const financialContext = buildFinancialContext(state);
    const attachmentContext = buildAttachmentContext(attachments);
    const contextWithAttachments = attachmentContext
        ? `${financialContext}\n\n### Attached Files\n${attachmentContext}`
        : financialContext;

    const template = isTransactionMode ? SYSTEM_PROMPT_TRANSACTION : SYSTEM_PROMPT_GENERAL;
    const systemPrompt = template.replace('{CONTEXT}', contextWithAttachments);

    const contextLength = await getModelContextLength();
    const reservedForPrompt = estimateTokens(systemPrompt) + estimateTokens(userMessage) + estimateTokens(attachmentContext) + 700;
    const historyBudget = Math.max(400, Math.min(1800, contextLength - reservedForPrompt));
    const safeHistory = trimChatHistory(chatHistory, historyBudget);

    const messages = [
        { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
        ...safeHistory.map(m => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content })),
        { role: 'user', content: userMessage },
    ];

    const response = await chatCompletion(messages, onToken, {
        maxTokens: 512,
        temperature: 0.7,
    });

    return response;
}


// ─── Unified send (local-only) ─
export async function sendChatMessage(userMessage, state, chatHistory = [], onToken, attachments = [], isTransactionMode = false) {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
        throw new Error('Local AI chatbot is only supported on iOS/Android.');
    }

    try {
        const response = await sendLocalChatMessage(userMessage, state, chatHistory, onToken, attachments, isTransactionMode);
        return { text: response, source: 'local' };
    } catch (error) {
        throw new Error(`Local AI unavailable: ${error.message}`);
    }
}


================ FILE: services/aiReceiptScanner.js ================

// AI Receipt Scanner Service
// LOCAL ONLY (on-device llama.rn)

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import { LOCAL_MODELS } from './localAIConfig';
import { getActiveModelConfig, getActiveModelId, isModelDownloaded, setActiveModelId } from './localAI';

const RECEIPT_CATEGORIES = new Set([
    'food', 'transport', 'daily_needs', 'health', 'shopping', 'entertainment',
    'education', 'bills', 'rent', 'beauty', 'gift', 'pets', 'other_spending',
]);

const NON_ITEM_KEYWORDS = new Set([
    'subtotal', 'service', 'tax', 'pajak', 'total', 'bayar', 'cash', 'voucher', 'kembalian',
    'change', 'discount', 'diskon', 'tip', 'rounding', 'pembulatan', 'meja', 'table', 'order',
    'bill', 'waiter', 'kasir', 'nama', 'no.bill', 'no. bill', 'tgl/jam', 'guest', 'tamu',
]);

const INVALID_RECEIPT_MESSAGE = 'This image is unclear or not a valid receipt. Please upload a clear receipt photo.';

function buildInvalidReceiptError() {
    return new Error(INVALID_RECEIPT_MESSAGE);
}



function isLikelyReceiptText(text) {
    const raw = extractTextValue(text);
    if (!raw) return false;

    const normalized = raw.replace(/\r/g, '\n');
    const lines = normalized.split('\n').map((line) => line.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (lines.length < 3) return false;

    const lower = normalized.toLowerCase();
    const hasReceiptKeywords = /(total|subtotal|tax|pajak|service|bayar|cash|debit|credit|kembalian|change|qty|quantity|tanggal|tgl|invoice|receipt|struk|no\.?\s*(bill|trx|transaksi)|rp|idr)/i.test(lower);
    const amountTokens = normalized.match(/\b\d{1,3}(?:[.,]\d{3})+(?:[.,]\d{2})?\b|\b\d{2,}\b/g) || [];
    const hasDate = !!parseDateFromText(normalized);

    return amountTokens.length >= 1 && (hasReceiptKeywords || hasDate);
}

function ensureValidReceiptResult(data) {
    if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') {
        throw buildInvalidReceiptError();
    }

    const items = Array.isArray(data.items) ? data.items : [];
    const pricedItems = items.filter((item) => {
        const name = extractTextValue(item?.name);
        const price = parseNumericValue(item?.price) || 0;
        return name.length >= 2 && price > 0;
    });

    const total = parseNumericValue(data.total) || 0;
    const subtotal = parseNumericValue(data.subtotal) || 0;
    const hasStructuredAmount = pricedItems.length > 0 || total > 0 || subtotal > 0;

    // Treat as invalid when there are no priced line-items and no meaningful totals.
    if (!hasStructuredAmount) {
        throw buildInvalidReceiptError();
    }
}

// ─── Receipt data structure helper ────────────
export function receiptToTransaction(receiptData) {
    const normalized = normalizeReceiptData(receiptData);
    const items = normalized.items;
    const total = normalized.total || items.reduce((s, i) => s + (i.price * i.quantity), 0);
    const mainCategory = items.length > 0 ? items[0].category : 'other_spending';

    return {
        type: 'spending',
        amount: total,
        category: mainCategory,
        description: normalized.storeName || 'Receipt Scan',
        date: normalized.date ? new Date(normalized.date).toISOString() : new Date().toISOString(),
        items,
        paymentMethod: normalized.paymentMethod || 'cash',
    };
}

function extractTextValue(value) {
    if (value == null) return '';
    if (typeof value === 'string' || typeof value === 'number' || typeof value === 'boolean') {
        return String(value).trim();
    }
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        return value.map(extractTextValue).filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim();
    }
    if (typeof value === 'object') {
        const preferred = [
            value.name, value.title, value.label, value.description, value.item,
            value.product, value.text, value.content, value.value, value.raw,
        ].map(extractTextValue).filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim();

        if (preferred) return preferred;

        return Object.values(value).map(extractTextValue).filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim();
    }
    return '';
}

function parseNumericString(raw) {
    if (!raw) return null;
    let cleaned = String(raw).replace(/[^0-9,.-]/g, '').trim();
    if (!cleaned) return null;

    if (cleaned.includes(',') && cleaned.includes('.')) {
        cleaned = cleaned.replace(/,/g, '');
    } else if (cleaned.includes(',')) {
        const parts = cleaned.split(',');
        const lastPart = parts[parts.length - 1];
        cleaned = lastPart.length === 2
            ? cleaned.replace(/\./g, '').replace(',', '.')
            : cleaned.replace(/,/g, '');
    }

    const matches = cleaned.match(/-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?/g);
    if (!matches || matches.length === 0) return null;

    const parsed = Number.parseFloat(matches[matches.length - 1]);
    return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : null;
}

const MONTH_NAME_MAP = {
    jan: '01', feb: '02', mar: '03', apr: '04', mei: '05', may: '05',
    jun: '06', jul: '07', agt: '08', aug: '08', sep: '09',
    okt: '10', oct: '10', nov: '11', des: '12', dec: '12',
    januari: '01', februari: '02', maret: '03', april: '04',
    juni: '06', juli: '07', agustus: '08', september: '09',
    oktober: '10', november: '11', desember: '12',
    january: '01', february: '02', march: '03', june: '06',
    july: '07', august: '08', october: '10', december: '12',
};

function parseDateFromText(text) {
    const raw = extractTextValue(text);
    if (!raw) return null;

    // Strip date label prefixes (Tanggal:, Tgl:, Date:, Tgl/Jam:, Waktu:)
    let stripped = raw.replace(/^(tanggal|tgl\/jam|tgl|date|waktu|time)\s*[:.\-]?\s*/i, '').trim();
    // Strip day-of-week prefixes (Indonesian and English)
    stripped = stripped.replace(/^(senin|selasa|rabu|kamis|jumat|sabtu|minggu|monday|tuesday|wednesday|thursday|friday|saturday|sunday)\s*[,.]?\s*/i, '').trim();

    // Named month: "17 Mar 2026", "17 Maret 2026", "17 March 2026" (with optional time)
    const named = stripped.match(/(\d{1,2})\s+([a-z]+)\s+(\d{4})(?:\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?)?/i);
    if (named) {
        const mm = MONTH_NAME_MAP[named[2].toLowerCase()];
        if (mm) {
            const dd = named[1].padStart(2, '0');
            const yyyy = named[3];
            const hh = (named[4] || '00').padStart(2, '0');
            const mi = (named[5] || '00').padStart(2, '0');
            const ss = (named[6] || '00').padStart(2, '0');
            return `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}T${hh}:${mi}:${ss}`;
        }
    }

    // Named month: "March 17, 2026" or "Mar 17 2026"
    const named2 = stripped.match(/([a-z]+)\s+(\d{1,2})[,\s]+(\d{4})/i);
    if (named2) {
        const mm = MONTH_NAME_MAP[named2[1].toLowerCase()];
        if (mm) {
            const dd = named2[2].padStart(2, '0');
            const yyyy = named2[3];
            return `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}T00:00:00`;
        }
    }

    // Numeric DMY: 17/03/2026, 17-03-26
    const dmy = stripped.match(/(\d{1,2})[\/-](\d{1,2})[\/-](\d{2,4})(?:\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?)?/);
    if (dmy) {
        const dd = dmy[1].padStart(2, '0');
        const mm = dmy[2].padStart(2, '0');
        const yyyy = dmy[3].length === 2 ? `20${dmy[3]}` : dmy[3];
        const hh = (dmy[4] || '00').padStart(2, '0');
        const mi = (dmy[5] || '00').padStart(2, '0');
        const ss = (dmy[6] || '00').padStart(2, '0');
        return `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}T${hh}:${mi}:${ss}`;
    }

    const ymd = stripped.match(/(\d{4})[\/-](\d{1,2})[\/-](\d{1,2})(?:\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?)?/);
    if (ymd) {
        const yyyy = ymd[1];
        const mm = ymd[2].padStart(2, '0');
        const dd = ymd[3].padStart(2, '0');
        const hh = (ymd[4] || '00').padStart(2, '0');
        const mi = (ymd[5] || '00').padStart(2, '0');
        const ss = (ymd[6] || '00').padStart(2, '0');
        return `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}T${hh}:${mi}:${ss}`;
    }

    return null;
}

function parseNumericValue(value) {
    if (value == null) return null;
    if (typeof value === 'number') return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
    if (typeof value === 'string') return parseNumericString(value);
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        for (const entry of value) {
            const parsed = parseNumericValue(entry);
            if (parsed != null) return parsed;
        }
        return null;
    }
    if (typeof value === 'object') {
        const preferredKeys = ['amount', 'total', 'price', 'value', 'subtotal', 'unitPrice', 'lineTotal', 'text', 'content', 'raw'];
        for (const key of preferredKeys) {
            if (value[key] != null) {
                const parsed = parseNumericValue(value[key]);
                if (parsed != null) return parsed;
            }
        }

        for (const entry of Object.values(value)) {
            const parsed = parseNumericValue(entry);
            if (parsed != null) return parsed;
        }
    }
    return null;
}

function normalizeCategory(value) {
    const category = extractTextValue(value).toLowerCase().trim();
    return RECEIPT_CATEGORIES.has(category) ? category : 'other_spending';
}

function normalizePaymentMethod(value) {
    const normalized = extractTextValue(value).toLowerCase();
    if (normalized.includes('cash')) return 'cash';
    if (normalized.includes('bank') || normalized.includes('card') || normalized.includes('debit') || normalized.includes('credit')) return 'bank';
    if (normalized.includes('wallet') || normalized.includes('ewallet') || normalized.includes('e-wallet') || normalized.includes('ovo') || normalized.includes('gopay') || normalized.includes('dana')) return 'ewallet';
    return normalized || 'cash';
}

function normalizeItem(item, index) {
    if (typeof item === 'string') {
        return {
            name: item.trim() || `Item ${index + 1}`,
            quantity: 1,
            price: 0,
            category: 'other_spending',
        };
    }

    const quantity = Math.max(1, parseNumericValue(item?.quantity ?? item?.qty ?? item?.count ?? item?.units ?? item?.cnt) || 1);
    const explicitPrice = parseNumericValue(item?.price ?? item?.unitPrice ?? item?.unit_price ?? item?.amount ?? item?.unitprice);
    const lineTotal = parseNumericValue(item?.lineTotal ?? item?.line_total ?? item?.total);
    const price = explicitPrice != null
        ? explicitPrice
        : lineTotal != null
            ? lineTotal / quantity
            : 0;

    return {
        name: extractTextValue(item?.name ?? item?.nm ?? item?.item ?? item?.description ?? item?.title ?? item?.product) || `Item ${index + 1}`,
        quantity,
        price: Number.isFinite(price) ? price : 0,
        category: normalizeCategory(item?.category),
    };
}

function toItemArray(value) {
    if (!value) return [];
    if (Array.isArray(value)) return value;
    if (typeof value === 'object') return Object.values(value);
    return [];
}

function summarizeResponse(content) {
    const summary = extractTextValue(content).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (!summary) return 'empty response';
    return summary.length > 180 ? `${summary.slice(0, 177)}...` : summary;
}

function normalizeReceiptData(parsed) {
    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        throw new Error('AI returned JSON in an unexpected receipt format.');
    }

    const rawItems = toItemArray(parsed.items)
        .concat(toItemArray(parsed.lineItems))
        .concat(toItemArray(parsed.products))
        .concat(toItemArray(parsed.menu))
        .concat(toItemArray(parsed.lines));

    const items = rawItems
        .map(normalizeItem)
        .filter((item) => extractTextValue(item?.name).length >= 2 && (item.price || 0) > 0);
    const itemsSubtotal = items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + ((item.price || 0) * (item.quantity || 1)), 0);
    const subtotal = parseNumericValue(parsed.subtotal) ?? itemsSubtotal;
    const tax = parseNumericValue(parsed.tax) ?? 0;
    const serviceCharge = parseNumericValue(parsed.serviceCharge ?? parsed.service_charge ?? parsed.service ?? parsed.servicecharge) ?? 0;
    const total = parseNumericValue(parsed.total)
        ?? parseNumericValue(parsed.grandTotal)
        ?? parseNumericValue(parsed.amount)
        ?? (subtotal + tax + serviceCharge);

    return {
        storeName: extractTextValue(parsed.storeName ?? parsed.merchantName ?? parsed.vendor ?? parsed.store ?? parsed.name) || 'Receipt Scan',
        date: extractTextValue(parsed.date ?? parsed.transactionDate ?? parsed.purchaseDate) || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
        items,
        subtotal,
        tax,
        serviceCharge,
        total: Number.isFinite(total) ? total : itemsSubtotal,
        paymentMethod: normalizePaymentMethod(parsed.paymentMethod ?? parsed.payment_method ?? parsed.method),
    };
}

// ─── JSON parsing helper (strips <think> blocks before parsing) ───────────────
function parseAIJSON(content) {
    const withoutThink = String(content || '')
        .replace(/<think>[\s\S]*?(?:<\/think>|$)/gi, '')
        .replace(/```json?\n?/gi, '')
        .replace(/```/g, '')
        .trim();
    const jsonMatch = withoutThink.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
    if (jsonMatch) {
        return JSON.parse(jsonMatch[0]);
    }
    return JSON.parse(withoutThink);
}

function parseLooseReceiptFromText(content) {
    const text = String(content || '');
    const items = [];

    const itemMatches = text.matchAll(/"(?:nm|name)"\s*:\s*"([^"]+)"[\s\S]{0,120}?"(?:unitprice|price|amount)"\s*:\s*"?([0-9.,]+)"?[\s\S]{0,80}?(?:"(?:cnt|qty|quantity)"\s*:\s*"?([0-9.,%]+)"?)?/gi);
    for (const match of itemMatches) {
        const rawName = (match[1] || '').trim();
        const rawPrice = match[2];
        const rawQty = match[3];
        if (!rawName) continue;

        const qty = Math.max(1, parseNumericValue(rawQty) || 1);
        const price = parseNumericValue(rawPrice) || 0;
        if (price <= 0) continue;

        items.push({
            name: rawName,
            quantity: qty,
            price,
            category: 'other_spending',
        });
    }

    const totalMatch = text.match(/"(?:total|grandTotal|amount)"\s*:\s*"?([0-9.,]+)"?/i);
    const total = parseNumericValue(totalMatch?.[1]) || items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + (i.price * i.quantity), 0);

    if (items.length === 0 && total <= 0) {
        throw new Error('AI response could not be converted into receipt items.');
    }

    return normalizeReceiptData({
        storeName: 'Receipt Scan',
        date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
        items,
        subtotal: items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + (i.price * i.quantity), 0),
        tax: 0,
        total,
        paymentMethod: 'cash',
    });
}

const RECEIPT_PROMPT = `Extract receipt image provided to JSON only. No markdown, no explanation.

JSON schema:
{
  "storeName": "string",
  "date": "YYYY-MM-DD",
  "items": [{"name": "string", "quantity": number, "price": number, "category": "food|transport|daily_needs|health|shopping|entertainment|education|bills|rent|beauty|gift|pets|other_spending"}],
  "subtotal": number,
  "serviceCharge": number,
  "tax": number,
  "total": number,
}

Rules:
- For quantity, check left of item name first, then right, if not available, default to 1

- Store name: usually on very top, skip addresses/phone/NPWP/websites. Sentence with "rd" or "st", or city name is not store name, DO NOT USE IT.
- Category: decide based on item name. If uncertain, use "other_spending"
- Date: ALL DATE MUST BE RETURNED AS YYYY-MM-DD on JSON format
- If the item name are in multiple languages, use English if available.
- Unclear or not a receipt image, return: {"invalidReceipt": true, "reason": "Not a valid receipt"}`;

// ─── FAST HYBRID AI SCAN: Native Hardware OCR (0.2s) -> Local LLM JSON (4s) ───
export async function scanReceiptLocal(input, onToken) {
    const { textCompletion, validateActiveModelForTask } = require('./localAI');
    const { runOCR } = require('./ocrService');
    validateActiveModelForTask('receipt-scan');

    // 1. Try Ultra-Fast Native Hardware OCR first (Apple Vision / Google ML Kit)
    // This avoids the 60-120s CPU bottleneck of mmproj-BF16 vision encoding!
    let ocrText = '';
    try {
        const ocrRes = await runOCR(input);
        ocrText = (ocrRes?.fullText || '').trim();
    } catch (ocrErr) {
        console.warn('[aiReceiptScanner] Native OCR pre-pass failed, falling back to vision:', ocrErr?.message);
    }

    if (ocrText.length >= 15) {
        return await scanReceiptFromText(ocrText, onToken);
    }

    // 2. Fallback to Direct Vision Multimodal only if Native OCR found no text
    const prompt = `${RECEIPT_PROMPT}\n\nAnalyze the receipt image and return JSON only.`;
    const response = await textCompletion(
        prompt,
        input,
        onToken,
        {
            maxTokens: 768,
            temperature: 0.1,
            disableThinking: true,
        }
    );

    try {
        const parsed = parseAIJSON(response);
        if (parsed?.invalidReceipt) {
            throw buildInvalidReceiptError();
        }
        const normalized = normalizeReceiptData(parsed);
        ensureValidReceiptResult(normalized);
        return normalized;
    } catch (e1) {
        if (e1.message === INVALID_RECEIPT_MESSAGE) throw e1;
        try {
            const normalized = parseLooseReceiptFromText(response);
            ensureValidReceiptResult(normalized);
            return normalized;
        } catch (e2) {
            if (e2.message === INVALID_RECEIPT_MESSAGE) throw e2;
            const rawPreview = typeof response === 'string' ? response.slice(0, 150).replace(/\n/g, ' ') : String(response);
            throw new Error(`AI returned invalid receipt JSON. Raw output: "${rawPreview}..."`);
        }
    }
}

// ─── TEXT-TO-JSON AI (Used by Hybrid AI Scan — 10x Faster than mmproj) ────────
export async function scanReceiptFromText(ocrText, onToken) {
    const { textCompletion } = require('./localAI');

    if (!ocrText || typeof ocrText !== 'string' || ocrText.trim().length < 10) {
        throw new Error('OCR text is too short to parse as a receipt.');
    }

    const trimmedOcr = ocrText.length > 3500 ? ocrText.slice(0, 3500) : ocrText;

    const prompt = `Extract this receipt OCR text into strict JSON only.
Schema:
{"storeName":"string","date":"YYYY-MM-DD","items":[{"name":"string","quantity":1,"price":0,"category":"food|transport|daily_needs|health|shopping|entertainment|education|bills|rent|beauty|gift|pets|other_spending"}],"subtotal":0,"serviceCharge":0,"tax":0,"total":0}

Rules:
- "price" is the total price for that item line. Keep full numbers (e.g. 75.000 or 75,000 -> 75000).
- Exclude subtotal, tax, PB1, PPN, service charge, cash/tunai, debit/qris, and change/kembali from "items".
- Convert the receipt date to YYYY-MM-DD.
- Output ONLY the JSON object.

Receipt OCR:
${trimmedOcr}`;

    const response = await textCompletion(
        prompt,
        null,
        onToken,
        {
            maxTokens: 768,
            temperature: 0.1,
            disableThinking: true,
        }
    );

    try {
        const parsed = parseAIJSON(response);
        if (parsed?.invalidReceipt) {
            throw buildInvalidReceiptError();
        }
        const normalized = normalizeReceiptData(parsed);
        ensureValidReceiptResult(normalized);
        return normalized;
    } catch (firstErr) {
        if (firstErr?.message === INVALID_RECEIPT_MESSAGE) throw firstErr;
        try {
            const normalized = parseLooseReceiptFromText(response);
            ensureValidReceiptResult(normalized);
            return normalized;
        } catch {
            // Final safety net: if LLM JSON was truncated, fall back to deterministic regex parser
            const { parseReceiptFromOCR } = require('./ocrService');
            const regexFallback = parseReceiptFromOCR(ocrText);
            if (regexFallback) return regexFallback;
            throw new Error(`AI returned invalid receipt JSON from OCR text. Response: ${summarizeResponse(response)}`);
        }
    }
}


// ─── Unified scan (local-only) ─
export async function scanReceipt(imageBase64OrText, apiKey, endpoint, onToken, originalUri = null, taskControl = null) {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
        throw new Error('Local AI receipt scanner is only supported on iOS/Android.');
    }

    const throwIfCancelled = () => taskControl?.throwIfCancelled?.();

    try {
        if (taskControl) {
            taskControl.onCancel?.(() => {
                const { stopCurrentCompletion } = require('./localAI');
                if (stopCurrentCompletion) stopCurrentCompletion();
            });
        }

        const localUri = originalUri || imageBase64OrText;
        if (!localUri || !(localUri.startsWith('file://') || localUri.startsWith('/'))) {
            throw new Error('Local receipt scan requires a local file URI from camera/gallery.');
        }

        throwIfCancelled();

        const parsed = await scanReceiptLocal(localUri, onToken);
        throwIfCancelled();
        ensureValidReceiptResult(parsed);
        return { data: parsed, source: 'local-image-direct' };
    } catch (error) {
        const msg = error?.message || 'Unknown local AI error';
        if (msg === INVALID_RECEIPT_MESSAGE || /invalidReceipt/i.test(msg) || /not a valid receipt/i.test(msg)) {
            throw buildInvalidReceiptError();
        }
        throw new Error(msg);
    }
}

================ FILE: services/appLogger.js ================

import * as FileSystem from 'expo-file-system';
import * as Sharing from 'expo-sharing';

const LOG_FILE_PATH = FileSystem.documentDirectory + 'app_activity_logs.json';
const MAX_LOGS = 500;

// In-memory listeners for live UI updates
const listeners = new Set();

const notifyListeners = (logs) => {
    listeners.forEach((cb) => {
        try {
            cb(logs);
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('[appLogger] Listener error:', e);
        }
    });
};

/**
 * Log any user activity or system event to local on-device storage.
 * @param {Object} entry
 * @param {('info'|'warn'|'error'|'success')} [entry.level='info']
 * @param {('transaction'|'asset'|'currency'|'settings'|'backup'|'ai'|'error'|'system')} [entry.category='system']
 * @param {string} entry.title
 * @param {string} [entry.details='']
 * @param {string} [entry.stack=null]
 */
export const logActivity = async ({
    level = 'info',
    category = 'system',
    title = '',
    details = '',
    stack = null,
} = {}) => {
    try {
        const newLog = {
            id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`,
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
            level,
            category,
            title,
            details: String(details || ''),
            stack: stack || null,
        };

        let logs = [];
        const fileInfo = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(LOG_FILE_PATH);
        if (fileInfo.exists) {
            const content = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(LOG_FILE_PATH);
            try {
                logs = JSON.parse(content);
                if (!Array.isArray(logs)) logs = [];
            } catch (e) {
                logs = [];
            }
        }

        logs.unshift(newLog);
        if (logs.length > MAX_LOGS) {
            logs = logs.slice(0, MAX_LOGS);
        }

        await FileSystem.writeAsStringAsync(LOG_FILE_PATH, JSON.stringify(logs, null, 2));
        notifyListeners(logs);
        console.log(`[appLogger] [${level.toUpperCase()}] [${category}] ${title} ${details ? '- ' + details : ''}`);
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('[appLogger] Failed to write activity log:', e);
    }
};

/**
 * Log an error or crash event
 */
export const logError = async (error, additionalInfo = '') => {
    const message = error?.message || String(error);
    const stack = error?.stack || null;
    return logActivity({
        level: 'error',
        category: 'error',
        title: 'Error Occurred',
        details: additionalInfo ? `${additionalInfo}: ${message}` : message,
        stack,
    });
};

/**
 * Retrieve all on-device logs
 * @returns {Promise<Array>}
 */
export const getLogs = async () => {
    try {
        const fileInfo = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(LOG_FILE_PATH);
        if (!fileInfo.exists) return [];
        const content = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(LOG_FILE_PATH);
        const parsed = JSON.parse(content);
        return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('[appLogger] Failed to read logs:', e);
        return [];
    }
};

/**
 * Clear all on-device logs
 */
export const clearLogs = async () => {
    try {
        const fileInfo = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(LOG_FILE_PATH);
        if (fileInfo.exists) {
            await FileSystem.deleteAsync(LOG_FILE_PATH);
        }
        notifyListeners([]);
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('[appLogger] Failed to clear logs:', e);
    }
};

/**
 * Export logs as a readable text file and trigger the native system share sheet
 */
export const exportLogs = async () => {
    try {
        const logs = await getLogs();
        if (logs.length === 0) {
            return { success: false, reason: 'empty' };
        }

        const lines = [
            '================================================================',
            'FinanceFlow On-Device Activity & System Log',
            `Exported at: ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`,
            `Total entries: ${logs.length}`,
            'Privacy notice: This log was generated locally on this device.',
            '================================================================\n',
        ];

        for (const log of logs) {
            lines.push(`[${log.timestamp}] [${log.level.toUpperCase()}] [${log.category.toUpperCase()}]`);
            lines.push(`Title: ${log.title}`);
            if (log.details) lines.push(`Details: ${log.details}`);
            if (log.stack) lines.push(`Stack: \n${log.stack}`);
            lines.push('----------------------------------------------------------------');
        }

        const exportPath = `${FileSystem.cacheDirectory}financeflow_logs_${Date.now()}.txt`;
        await FileSystem.writeAsStringAsync(exportPath, lines.join('\n'));

        if (await Sharing.isAvailableAsync()) {
            await Sharing.shareAsync(exportPath, {
                mimeType: 'text/plain',
                dialogTitle: 'Export FinanceFlow Logs',
                UTI: 'public.plain-text',
            });
            return { success: true };
        } else {
            return { success: false, reason: 'sharing_unavailable' };
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('[appLogger] Export failed:', e);
        return { success: false, reason: e.message };
    }
};

/**
 * Subscribe to live log updates
 */
export const subscribeToLogs = (callback) => {
    listeners.add(callback);
    return () => listeners.delete(callback);
};

import { enqueueReceiptScan, enqueuePdfImport, taskManager } from './backgroundTaskManager';

 *       setScannedData(result);
 *     }

 * ```

export async function scanReceiptInBackground(imageUri, scanFunction) {

        name: 'Scan Receipt',
        description: 'Processing receipt image in background...',
    });
}

export async function importPdfInBackground(filePath, importFunction) {

// ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

// ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

/**
 * Process multiple PDFs in batch
 * Each PDF is queued as a separate task
 */
export async function importMultiplePdfsInBackground(filePaths, importFunction) {
    const taskIds = filePaths.map((filePath, index) =>
        enqueuePdfImport(filePath, importFunction, {
            name: `Import PDF ${index + 1}/${filePaths.length}`,
            description: `Processing ${filePath.split('/').pop()}...`,
        })
    );
    return taskIds;
}

/**
 * Wait for all tasks to complete
 */
export async function waitForTaskCompletion(taskIds, timeout = 30 * 60 * 1000) {
    return Promise.race([
        new Promise((resolve) => {
            let completed = 0;
            const results = {};

            taskIds.forEach(id => {
                taskManager.onTaskUpdate((taskId, status, progress, result, error) => {
                    if (!taskIds.includes(taskId)) return;

                    if (status === 'completed' || status === 'failed' || status === 'cancelled') {
                        results[taskId] = { status, result, error };
                        completed++;

                        if (completed === taskIds.length) {
                            resolve(results);
                        }
                    }
                });
            });
        }),
        new Promise((_, reject) =>
            setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Task completion timeout')), timeout)
        ),
    ]);
}

export function getActiveTasks() {
    return taskManager.getActiveTasks();
}

/**

export function getTaskProgress(taskId) {
    const task = taskManager.getTask(taskId);
    return task ? task.progress : 0;
}

/**
 * Check if a task is still running
 */
export function isTaskRunning(taskId) {
    const task = taskManager.getTask(taskId);
    return task ? ['pending', 'running'].includes(task.status) : false;
}

    return taskManager.cancelTask(taskId);

    return taskManager.exportHistory();

================ FILE: services/backgroundTaskManager.js ================

// Background Task Manager for long-running operations (receipt scanning, PDF import, etc.)
// Provides async task queuing, progress tracking, and notifications

import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { router } from 'expo-router';

const TASK_STORAGE_KEY = 'bg_tasks';
const TASK_HISTORY_KEY = 'bg_task_history';

// Task status enum
export const TaskStatus = {
    PENDING: 'pending',
    RUNNING: 'running',
    COMPLETED: 'completed',
    FAILED: 'failed',
    CANCELLED: 'cancelled',
};

// Task type enum
export const TaskType = {
    SCAN_RECEIPT: 'scan_receipt',
    IMPORT_PDF: 'import_pdf',
    BATCH_IMPORT: 'batch_import',
};

class BackgroundTaskManager {
    constructor() {
        this.queue = [];
        this.running = false;
        this.listeners = [];
        this.currentTask = null;
        this.taskResults = new Map();
    }

    /**
     * Register a listener for task status updates
     * @param {Function} callback - Called with (taskId, status, progress, result, error)
     */
    onTaskUpdate(callback) {
        this.listeners.push(callback);
        return () => {
            this.listeners = this.listeners.filter(l => l !== callback);
        };
    }

    /**
     * Emit task update to all listeners
     */
    _notifyListeners(taskId, status, progress = 0, result = null, error = null) {
        this.listeners.forEach(callback => {
            try {
                callback(taskId, status, progress, result, error);
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Task listener error:', err);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Create and queue a new task
     * @param {string} type - Task type from TaskType enum
     * @param {Function} handler - Async function that performs the task
     * @param {Object} metadata - Task metadata (name, description, etc)
     * @returns {string} Task ID
     */
    enqueueTask(type, handler, metadata = {}) {
        const taskId = `task_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}`;
        const task = {
            id: taskId,
            type,
            handler,
            status: TaskStatus.PENDING,
            progress: 0,
            result: null,
            error: null,
            cancelRequested: false,
            cancellationReason: null,
            metadata: {
                name: metadata.name || type,
                description: metadata.description || '',
                createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                ...metadata,
            },
            startedAt: null,
            completedAt: null,
        };

        this.queue.push(task);
        this._notifyListeners(taskId, TaskStatus.PENDING, 0);
        this._processQueue();
        return taskId;
    }

    /**
     * Get task info
     */
    getTask(taskId) {
        if (this.currentTask && this.currentTask.id === taskId) {
            return { ...this.currentTask };
        }
        const queued = this.queue.find(t => t.id === taskId);
        if (queued) return { ...queued };
        return this.taskResults.get(taskId) || null;
    }

    /**
     * Get all tasks (pending, running, and completed)
     */
    getAllTasks() {
        const tasks = [
            ...this.queue,
            ...Array.from(this.taskResults.values()),
        ];
        if (this.currentTask) tasks.push(this.currentTask);
        return tasks.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.metadata.createdAt) - new Date(a.metadata.createdAt));
    }

    /**
     * Get only pending/running tasks
     */
    getActiveTasks() {
        return this.queue
            .concat(this.currentTask ? [this.currentTask] : [])
            .filter(t => !t.cancelRequested && t.status !== TaskStatus.CANCELLED);
    }

    /**
     * Cancel a pending task
     */
    cancelTask(taskId) {
        const idx = this.queue.findIndex(t => t.id === taskId);
        if (idx >= 0) {
            const task = this.queue[idx];
            task.status = TaskStatus.CANCELLED;
            task.cancelRequested = true;
            task.cancellationReason = 'Cancelled before start';
            task.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
            this.queue.splice(idx, 1);
            this.taskResults.set(task.id, task);
            this._notifyListeners(taskId, TaskStatus.CANCELLED);
            return true;
        }

        if (this.currentTask && this.currentTask.id === taskId) {
            this.currentTask.cancelRequested = true;
            this.currentTask.cancellationReason = 'Cancelled by user';
            this.currentTask.status = TaskStatus.CANCELLED;
            if (typeof this.currentTask._cancelFn === 'function') {
                this.currentTask._cancelFn(new Error('Task cancelled by user'));
            }
            if (typeof this.currentTask._cancelCallback === 'function') {
                try { this.currentTask._cancelCallback(); } catch(e) {}
            }
            this._notifyListeners(taskId, TaskStatus.CANCELLED, this.currentTask.progress || 0);
            return true;
        }

        return false;
    }

    /**
     * Clear completed tasks from history
     */
    clearHistory() {
        const before = this.taskResults.size;
        this.taskResults.clear();
        return before;
    }

    /**
     * Process the task queue sequentially
     */
    async _processQueue() {
        if (this.running || this.queue.length === 0) {
            return;
        }

        this.running = true;
        while (this.queue.length > 0) {
            const task = this.queue.shift();
            this.currentTask = task;
            let cancelTaskExecution = null;

            try {
                task.status = TaskStatus.RUNNING;
                task.startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
                this._notifyListeners(task.id, TaskStatus.RUNNING, 0);

                // Create a progress callback function for handlers to use
                const reportProgress = (progress) => {
                    if (task.cancelRequested || task.status === TaskStatus.CANCELLED) return;
                    task.progress = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, progress));
                    this._notifyListeners(task.id, TaskStatus.RUNNING, task.progress);
                };

                const taskControl = {
                    isCancelled: () => !!task.cancelRequested,
                    throwIfCancelled: () => {
                        if (task.cancelRequested) {
                            throw new Error('Task cancelled by user');
                        }
                    },
                    onCancel: (fn) => {
                        task._cancelCallback = fn;
                    }
                };

                const cancelPromise = new Promise((_, reject) => {
                    cancelTaskExecution = reject;
                });
                task._cancelFn = cancelTaskExecution;

                // Execute the task with timeout protection (30 minutes max)
                const result = await Promise.race([
                    task.handler(reportProgress, taskControl).catch(e => {
                        if (task.cancelRequested) return null;
                        throw e;
                    }),
                    new Promise((_, reject) =>
                        setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Task timeout (30m)')), 30 * 60 * 1000)
                    ),
                    cancelPromise,
                ]);

                if (task.cancelRequested) {
                    task.status = TaskStatus.CANCELLED;
                    task.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
                    this.taskResults.set(task.id, task);
                    this._notifyListeners(task.id, TaskStatus.CANCELLED, task.progress || 0);
                    continue;
                }

                task.status = TaskStatus.COMPLETED;
                task.result = result;
                task.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
                this.taskResults.set(task.id, task);
                this._notifyListeners(task.id, TaskStatus.COMPLETED, 100, result);

            } catch (error) {
                if (task.cancelRequested || /task cancelled/i.test(String(error?.message || ''))) {
                    task.status = TaskStatus.CANCELLED;
                    task.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
                    this.taskResults.set(task.id, task);
                    this._notifyListeners(task.id, TaskStatus.CANCELLED, task.progress || 0);
                } else {
                    task.status = TaskStatus.FAILED;
                    task.error = {
                        message: error.message,
                        stack: error.stack,
                        code: error.code,
                    };
                    task.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
                    this.taskResults.set(task.id, task);
                    console.error(`Task ${task.id} failed:`, error);
                    this._notifyListeners(task.id, TaskStatus.FAILED, 0, null, task.error);
                }
            } finally {
                task._cancelFn = null;
                cancelTaskExecution = null;
                this.currentTask = null;
            }
        }

        this.running = false;
    }

    /**
     * Export task history to JSON for debugging
     */
    async exportHistory() {
        const tasks = this.getAllTasks();
        return {
            exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
            taskCount: tasks.length,
            tasks: tasks.map(t => ({
                ...t,
                handler: undefined, // Don't export function
            })),
        };
    }
}

// Singleton instance
export const taskManager = new BackgroundTaskManager();

// Notification Service (using native alerts and toasts)
// ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━████████

import { Alert } from 'react-native';

export class NotificationService {
    /**
     * Show a toast-like notification (uses native Alert for now)
     */
    static showNotification(title, message, duration = 3000) {
        Alert.alert(title, message, [{ text: 'OK' }]);
    }

    /**
     * Show task progress notification
     */
    static showTaskProgress(taskMetadata, progress) {
        // In a real app, you might use react-native-toasts or similar
        // For now, just log it
        console.log(`Task: ${taskMetadata.name} - ${progress}%`);
    }

    /**
     * Show task completion notification
     */
    static showTaskComplete(taskMetadata, result) {
        const message = taskMetadata.description
            ? `${taskMetadata.description}\n✓ Completed`
            : 'Task completed successfully';

        if (taskMetadata.type === TaskType.SCAN_RECEIPT) {
            Alert.alert('Success', 'Receipt scanned successfully.', [
                { text: 'Later', style: 'cancel' },
                {
                    text: 'Open Scan',
                    onPress: () => {
                        try {
                            router.push('/receipt-scanner');
                        } catch {
                            // Navigation best effort only.
                        }
                    },
                },
            ]);
            return;
        }

        this.showNotification('Success', message);
    }

    /**
     * Show task failure notification
     */
    static showTaskFailed(taskMetadata, error) {
        const message = `${taskMetadata.name} failed:\n${error.message}`;
        this.showNotification('Error', message);
    }
}

// ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━████████
// Higher-level task creation helpers
// ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━████████

/**
 * Enqueue a receipt scanning task
 * @param {Object} input - Receipt image input (uri or base64)
 * @param {Function} scanFunction - The async scan function to call
 * @param {Object} options - Additional options
 */
export function enqueueReceiptScan(input, scanFunction, options = {}) {
    const taskId = taskManager.enqueueTask(
        TaskType.SCAN_RECEIPT,
        async (reportProgress, taskControl) => {
            reportProgress(10);
            try {
                const result = await scanFunction(input, (token) => {
                    // Update progress based on token streaming
                    reportProgress(Math.min(90, 10 + token.length / 100));
                }, taskControl);
                reportProgress(100);
                return result;
            } catch (err) {
                throw new Error(`Receipt scan failed: ${err.message}`);
            }
        },
        {
            name: options.name || 'Scan Receipt',
            description: options.description || 'Processing receipt image...',
        }
    );

    // Auto-notify on completion
    taskManager.onTaskUpdate((id, status, progress, result, error) => {
        if (id !== taskId) return;

        if (status === TaskStatus.COMPLETED) {
            const { updateReceiptScanSession } = require('./receiptScanSession');
            updateReceiptScanSession({
                taskId,
                status: 'completed',
                result: result?.data || result,
                error: null,
            });
            NotificationService.showTaskComplete(
                { name: 'Receipt Scan', description: 'Receipt scanned successfully', type: TaskType.SCAN_RECEIPT },
                result
            );
        } else if (status === TaskStatus.FAILED) {
            const { updateReceiptScanSession } = require('./receiptScanSession');
            updateReceiptScanSession({
                taskId,
                status: 'failed',
                error: error?.message || 'Receipt scan failed',
            });
            NotificationService.showTaskFailed(
                { name: 'Receipt Scan' },
                error
            );
        } else if (status === TaskStatus.CANCELLED) {
            const { updateReceiptScanSession } = require('./receiptScanSession');
            updateReceiptScanSession({
                taskId: null,
                status: 'cancelled',
                result: null,
            });
        }
    });

    return taskId;
}

/**
 * Enqueue a PDF import task
 * @param {string} filePath - Path to PDF file
 * @param {Function} importFunction - The async import function to call
 * @param {Object} options - Additional options
 */
export function enqueuePdfImport(filePath, importFunction, options = {}) {
    const taskId = taskManager.enqueueTask(
        TaskType.IMPORT_PDF,
        async (reportProgress, taskControl) => {
            reportProgress(10);
            try {
                const result = await importFunction(filePath, (stage, progress) => {
                    // Update progress based on stage
                    const stageProgress = {
                        extracting: 10,
                        parsing: 40,
                        validating: 80,
                    };
                    const baseProgress = stageProgress[stage] || 10;
                    reportProgress(baseProgress + (progress || 0));
                }, taskControl);
                reportProgress(100);
                return result;
            } catch (err) {
                throw new Error(`PDF import failed: ${err.message}`);
            }
        },
        {
            name: options.name || 'Import PDF',
            description: options.description || 'Processing PDF file...',
        }
    );

    // Auto-notify on completion
    taskManager.onTaskUpdate((id, status, progress, result, error) => {
        if (id !== taskId) return;

        if (status === TaskStatus.COMPLETED) {
            NotificationService.showTaskComplete(
                {
                    name: 'PDF Import',
                    description: `Imported ${result?.transactionCount || 0} transactions`,
                },
                result
            );
        } else if (status === TaskStatus.FAILED) {
            NotificationService.showTaskFailed(
                { name: 'PDF Import' },
                error
            );
        }
    });

    return taskId;
}


================ FILE: services/dataExport.js ================

// Data Export / Import Service
// Supports JSON and Excel (XLSX) formats

import * as FileSystem from 'expo-file-system';
import * as Sharing from 'expo-sharing';
import * as XLSX from 'xlsx';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import * as db from './database';
import { getOrFetchRates, convertCurrency } from './exchangeRates';
import { generateId } from '../utils/helpers';

function normalizeSavingHistory(goal) {
    if (Array.isArray(goal?.savingHistory)) return goal.savingHistory;
    if (Array.isArray(goal?.savingsHistory)) return goal.savingsHistory;
    return [];
}

function getFilterInterval(options) {
    const { period, start, end } = options || {};
    if (!period || period === 'all') return null;

    const now = new Date();
    let startDate, endDate;

    if (period === 'daily') {
        startDate = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), 0, 0, 0);
        endDate = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), 23, 59, 59, 999);
    } else if (period === 'weekly') {
        const day = now.getDay();
        const diff = now.getDate() - day + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1);
        startDate = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), diff, 0, 0, 0);
        endDate = new Date(startDate.getTime() + 6 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 + 23 * 60 * 60 * 1000 + 59 * 60 * 1000 + 59 * 1000 + 999);
    } else if (period === 'monthly') {
        startDate = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1, 0, 0, 0);
        endDate = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() + 1, 0, 23, 59, 59, 999);
    } else if (period === 'custom') {
        if (!start || !end) return null;
        startDate = new Date(`${start}T00:00:00Z`);
        endDate = new Date(`${end}T23:59:59Z`);
    }

    return { startDate, endDate };
}

// ─── EXPORT ───────────────────────────────────

export async function exportToJSON() {
    const data = await db.getAllData();

    // Load AsyncStorage settings/appearance preferences
    let appThemeMode = 'system';
    let thinkingPrefs = null;
    let activeModelId = null;
    let modelContextOverrides = null;

    try {
        appThemeMode = (await AsyncStorage.getItem('app_theme_mode')) || 'system';
        const rawThinking = await AsyncStorage.getItem('@financeflow_ai_thinking_prefs_v1');
        if (rawThinking) thinkingPrefs = JSON.parse(rawThinking);
        activeModelId = await AsyncStorage.getItem('active_model_id');
        const rawOverrides = await AsyncStorage.getItem('model_context_overrides');
        if (rawOverrides) modelContextOverrides = JSON.parse(rawOverrides);
    } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Failed to load some settings from AsyncStorage during export:', e);
    }

    const sanitizedSettings = { ...(data.settings || {}) };
    delete sanitizedSettings.aiApiKey;
    delete sanitizedSettings.aiEndpoint;

    const exportData = {
        version: '3.0',
        exportDate: new Date().toISOString(),
        app: 'FinanceFlow',
        data: {
            transactions: data.transactions,
            budgets: data.budgets,
            assets: data.assets,
            savingsGoals: data.savingsGoals || [],
            chatSessions: data.chatSessions || [],
            vehicles: data.vehicles || [],
            recurringTransactions: data.recurringTransactions || [],
            settings: {
                ...sanitizedSettings,
                app_theme_mode: appThemeMode,
                ai_thinking_prefs: thinkingPrefs,
                active_model_id: activeModelId,
                model_context_overrides: modelContextOverrides,
            },
        },
    };

    const json = JSON.stringify(exportData, null, 2);
    const fileName = `financeflow_export_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.json`;
    const filePath = `${FileSystem.cacheDirectory}${fileName}`;

    await FileSystem.writeAsStringAsync(filePath, json);
    await Sharing.shareAsync(filePath, {
        mimeType: 'application/json',
        dialogTitle: 'Export FinanceFlow Data',
        UTI: 'public.json',
    });

    return filePath;
}

export async function exportToExcel(options = {}) {
    const data = await db.getAllData();
    const interval = getFilterInterval(options);

    let transactions = data.transactions || [];
    if (interval) {
        transactions = transactions.filter(t => {
            const txDate = new Date(t.date);
            return txDate >= interval.startDate && txDate <= interval.endDate;
        });
    }

    const baseCurrency = data.settings?.currency || 'USD';
    const exchangeRates = await getOrFetchRates(baseCurrency);

    // --- Generate Summary Dashboard Data (converted to baseCurrency) ---
    const toBase = (t) => Math.abs(convertCurrency(t.amount || 0, t.currency || baseCurrency, baseCurrency, exchangeRates));
    const totalIncome = transactions.filter(t => t.type === 'income').reduce((sum, t) => sum + toBase(t), 0);
    const totalExpense = transactions.filter(t => t.type === 'spending').reduce((sum, t) => sum + toBase(t), 0);

    const categoryTotals = {};
    transactions.filter(t => t.type === 'spending').forEach(t => {
        categoryTotals[t.category] = (categoryTotals[t.category] || 0) + toBase(t);
    });
    const sortedCategories = Object.keys(categoryTotals).sort((a, b) => categoryTotals[b] - categoryTotals[a]);

    const summaryRows = [
        { 'Metric': 'Total Income', 'Amount': totalIncome },
        { 'Metric': 'Total Spending', 'Amount': totalExpense },
        {}, // blank row
        { 'Metric': 'Spending by Category', 'Amount': '' }
    ];
    sortedCategories.forEach(cat => {
        summaryRows.push({ 'Metric': cat, 'Amount': categoryTotals[cat] });
    });

    // Transactions sheet
    const txRows = transactions.map(t => {
        let paymentMethodDisplay = '';
        if (t.paymentMethod === 'transfer') {
            paymentMethodDisplay = 'Transfer';
        } else if (t.paymentMethod === 'asset' || t.assetId) {
            const asset = data.assets.find(a => a.id === t.assetId);
            paymentMethodDisplay = asset ? asset.name : 'Account';
        } else {
            paymentMethodDisplay = '';
        }

        return {
            Id: t.id,
            Date: t.date,
            Type: t.type,
            Amount: t.amount,
            Category: t.category,
            Description: t.description || '',
            'Payment Method': paymentMethodDisplay,
            Currency: t.currency || baseCurrency,
            'Converted Amount': convertCurrency(t.amount, t.currency || baseCurrency, baseCurrency, exchangeRates),
            'Base Currency': baseCurrency,
            'Exchange Rate': convertCurrency(1, t.currency || baseCurrency, baseCurrency, exchangeRates),
            'Created At': t.createdAt || '',
            'Asset Id': t.assetId || '',
            'From Asset Id': t.fromAssetId || '',
            'To Asset Id': t.toAssetId || '',
            Tax: t.tax || 0,
            'Tax Is Percent': t.taxIsPercent ? 'true' : 'false',
            'Tax Enabled': t.taxEnabled ? 'true' : 'false',
            'Service Fee': t.serviceFee || 0,
            'Service Fee Is Percent': t.serviceFeeIsPercent ? 'true' : 'false',
            'Service Fee Enabled': t.serviceFeeEnabled ? 'true' : 'false',
            Tip: t.tip || 0,
            'Tip Is Percent': t.tipIsPercent ? 'true' : 'false',
            'Tip Enabled': t.tipEnabled ? 'true' : 'false',
            Items: JSON.stringify(t.items || []),
            Photos: JSON.stringify(t.photos || []),
            'Vehicle Id': t.vehicleId || '',
            'Vehicle Log Type': t.vehicleLogType || '',
            'Vehicle Odometer': t.vehicleOdometer || '',
            'Vehicle Volume': t.vehicleVolume || '',
            'Vehicle Maintenance Items': t.vehicleMaintenanceItems || '',
        };
    });

    // Budgets sheet
    const budgetRows = data.budgets.map(b => ({
        Id: b.id,
        Period: b.period,
        Amount: b.amount,
        Category: b.category || 'all',
        Currency: b.currency || baseCurrency,
        'Converted Amount': convertCurrency(b.amount, b.currency || baseCurrency, baseCurrency, exchangeRates),
        'Base Currency': baseCurrency,
        'Created At': b.createdAt || '',
    }));

    // Assets sheet
    const assetRows = data.assets.map(a => ({
        Id: a.id,
        Name: a.name,
        Type: a.type,
        Balance: a.balance,
        Color: a.color || '',
        Currency: a.currency || baseCurrency,
        'Converted Balance': convertCurrency(a.balance, a.currency || baseCurrency, baseCurrency, exchangeRates),
        'Base Currency': baseCurrency,
        'Created At': a.createdAt || '',
    }));

    // Saving Goals sheet
    const goalRows = (data.savingsGoals || []).map(g => ({
        Id: g.id,
        Title: g.title,
        'Target Amount': g.targetAmount,
        'Current Amount': g.currentAmount || 0,
        'Start Date': g.startDate || '',
        'End Date': g.endDate || '',
        Commitment: g.commitment || '',
        'Notify Enabled': g.notifyEnabled ? 'true' : 'false',
        'Is Completed': g.isCompleted ? 'true' : 'false',
        Currency: g.currency || baseCurrency,
        'Converted Target': convertCurrency(g.targetAmount, g.currency || baseCurrency, baseCurrency, exchangeRates),
        'Converted Current': convertCurrency(g.currentAmount, g.currency || baseCurrency, baseCurrency, exchangeRates),
        'Base Currency': baseCurrency,
        'Created At': g.createdAt || '',
    }));

    // Saving History sheet
    const historyRows = [];
    for (const g of (data.savingsGoals || [])) {
        for (const h of normalizeSavingHistory(g)) {
            historyRows.push({
                'Goal Id': g.id,
                'Goal Title': g.title,
                'Entry Id': h.id,
                Amount: h.amount,
                Date: h.date,
                'Created At': h.createdAt || '',
            });
        }
    }

    // Chat Sessions sheet
    const chatRows = (data.chatSessions || []).map(s => ({
        Id: s.id,
        Title: s.title || 'Chat',
        Messages: JSON.stringify(s.messages || []),
        'Created At': s.createdAt || '',
        'Updated At': s.updatedAt || '',
    }));

    // Vehicles sheet
    const vehicleRows = (data.vehicles || []).map(v => ({
        Id: v.id,
        Name: v.name,
        Type: v.type || 'gas',
        Currency: v.currency || 'USD',
        'Initial Odometer': v.initialOdometer || 0,
        'Created At': v.createdAt || '',
    }));

    const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();

    const wsSummary = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(summaryRows);
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsSummary, 'Summary Dashboard');

    const wsTx = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(txRows);
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsTx, 'Transactions');

    const wsBudget = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(budgetRows);
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsBudget, 'Budgets');

    const wsAssets = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(assetRows);
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsAssets, 'Assets');

    const wsGoals = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(goalRows);
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsGoals, 'Saving Goals');

    const wsHistory = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(historyRows);
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsHistory, 'Saving History');

    const wsChats = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(chatRows);
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsChats, 'Chat Sessions');

    const wsVehicles = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(vehicleRows);
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsVehicles, 'Vehicles');

    // Write to file
    const wbout = XLSX.write(wb, { type: 'base64', bookType: 'xlsx' });
    const fileName = `financeflow_export_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.xlsx`;
    const filePath = `${FileSystem.cacheDirectory}${fileName}`;

    await FileSystem.writeAsStringAsync(filePath, wbout, {
        encoding: FileSystem.EncodingType.Base64,
    });

    await Sharing.shareAsync(filePath, {
        mimeType: 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet',
        dialogTitle: 'Export FinanceFlow Data',
    });

    return filePath;
}

// ─── IMPORT ───────────────────────────────────

export async function importFromJSON(fileUri) {
    const content = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(fileUri);
    const parsed = JSON.parse(content);

    // Validate structure
    if (!parsed.data) {
        throw new Error('Invalid file format. Expected FinanceFlow export file.');
    }

    const result = {
        transactions: [],
        budgets: [],
        assets: [],
        savingsGoals: [],
        chatSessions: [],
        vehicles: [],
        settings: parsed.data.settings || {},
    };

    if (parsed.data.transactions && Array.isArray(parsed.data.transactions)) {
        result.transactions = parsed.data.transactions.map(t => ({
            id: t.id || generateId(),
            type: t.type || 'spending',
            amount: parseFloat(t.amount) || 0,
            category: t.category || 'other_spending',
            description: t.description || '',
            date: t.date || new Date().toISOString(),
            items: Array.isArray(t.items) ? t.items : [],
            photos: Array.isArray(t.photos) ? t.photos : [],
            paymentMethod: t.paymentMethod || 'cash',
            assetId: t.assetId || null,
            fromAssetId: t.fromAssetId || null,
            toAssetId: t.toAssetId || null,
            tax: parseFloat(t.tax) || 0,
            taxIsPercent: !!t.taxIsPercent,
            taxEnabled: !!t.taxEnabled,
            serviceFee: parseFloat(t.serviceFee) || 0,
            serviceFeeIsPercent: !!t.serviceFeeIsPercent,
            serviceFeeEnabled: !!t.serviceFeeEnabled,
            tip: parseFloat(t.tip) || 0,
            tipIsPercent: !!t.tipIsPercent,
            tipEnabled: !!t.tipEnabled,
            currency: t.currency || 'USD',
            createdAt: t.createdAt || new Date().toISOString(),
            vehicleId: t.vehicleId || null,
            vehicleLogType: t.vehicleLogType || null,
            vehicleOdometer: t.vehicleOdometer ? parseFloat(t.vehicleOdometer) : null,
            vehicleVolume: t.vehicleVolume ? parseFloat(t.vehicleVolume) : null,
            vehicleMaintenanceItems: t.vehicleMaintenanceItems || null,
        }));
    }

    if (parsed.data.vehicles && Array.isArray(parsed.data.vehicles)) {
        result.vehicles = parsed.data.vehicles.map(v => ({
            id: v.id || generateId(),
            name: v.name || 'Unknown Vehicle',
            type: v.type || 'gas',
            currency: v.currency || 'USD',
            initialOdometer: parseFloat(v.initialOdometer) || 0,
            createdAt: v.createdAt || new Date().toISOString(),
        }));
    }

    if (parsed.data.budgets && Array.isArray(parsed.data.budgets)) {
        result.budgets = parsed.data.budgets.map(b => ({
            id: b.id || generateId(),
            period: b.period || 'monthly',
            amount: parseFloat(b.amount) || 0,
            currency: b.currency || 'USD',
            createdAt: b.createdAt || new Date().toISOString(),
        }));
    }

    if (parsed.data.assets && Array.isArray(parsed.data.assets)) {
        result.assets = parsed.data.assets.map(a => ({
            id: a.id || generateId(),
            name: a.name || 'Unknown',
            type: a.type || 'bank',
            balance: parseFloat(a.balance) || 0,
            color: a.color || null,
            currency: a.currency || 'USD',
            createdAt: a.createdAt || new Date().toISOString(),
        }));
    }

    if (parsed.data.savingsGoals && Array.isArray(parsed.data.savingsGoals)) {
        result.savingsGoals = parsed.data.savingsGoals.map(g => ({
            id: g.id || generateId(),
            title: g.title || 'Untitled Goal',
            targetAmount: parseFloat(g.targetAmount) || 0,
            currentAmount: parseFloat(g.currentAmount) || 0,
            startDate: g.startDate || null,
            endDate: g.endDate || null,
            commitment: g.commitment || null,
            notifyEnabled: !!g.notifyEnabled,
            savingHistory: normalizeSavingHistory(g),
            isCompleted: !!g.isCompleted,
            currency: g.currency || 'USD',
            createdAt: g.createdAt || new Date().toISOString(),
        }));
    }

    if (parsed.data.chatSessions && Array.isArray(parsed.data.chatSessions)) {
        result.chatSessions = parsed.data.chatSessions.map(s => ({
            id: s.id || generateId(),
            title: s.title || 'New Chat',
            messages: Array.isArray(s.messages) ? s.messages : [],
            createdAt: s.createdAt || new Date().toISOString(),
            updatedAt: s.updatedAt || new Date().toISOString(),
        }));
    }

    return result;
}

export async function importFromExcel(fileUri) {
    const base64 = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(fileUri, {
        encoding: FileSystem.EncodingType.Base64,
    });

    const wb = XLSX.read(base64, { type: 'base64' });
    const result = { transactions: [], budgets: [], assets: [], savingsGoals: [], chatSessions: [] };

    // Parse Assets sheet
    if (wb.SheetNames.includes('Assets')) {
        const ws = wb.Sheets['Assets'];
        const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws);
        result.assets = rows.map(r => ({
            id: r.Id || generateId(),
            name: r.Name || 'Unknown',
            type: (r.Type || 'bank').toLowerCase(),
            balance: parseFloat(r.Balance) || 0,
            color: r.Color || null,
            currency: r.Currency || 'USD',
            createdAt: r['Created At'] || new Date().toISOString(),
        }));
    }

    // Parse Budgets sheet
    if (wb.SheetNames.includes('Budgets')) {
        const ws = wb.Sheets['Budgets'];
        const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws);
        result.budgets = rows.map(r => ({
            id: r.Id || generateId(),
            period: (r.Period || 'monthly').toLowerCase(),
            amount: parseFloat(r.Amount) || 0,
            category: r.Category || 'all',
            currency: r.Currency || 'USD',
            createdAt: r['Created At'] || new Date().toISOString(),
        }));
    }

    // Parse Saving Goals sheet
    if (wb.SheetNames.includes('Saving Goals') || wb.SheetNames.includes('SavingGoals')) {
        const sheetName = wb.SheetNames.includes('Saving Goals') ? 'Saving Goals' : 'SavingGoals';
        const ws = wb.Sheets[sheetName];
        const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws);
        result.savingsGoals = rows.map(r => ({
            id: r.Id || generateId(),
            title: r.Title || 'Untitled Goal',
            targetAmount: parseFloat(r['Target Amount']) || 0,
            currentAmount: parseFloat(r['Current Amount']) || 0,
            startDate: r['Start Date'] || null,
            endDate: r['End Date'] || null,
            commitment: r.Commitment || null,
            notifyEnabled: r['Notify Enabled'] === 'true' || r['Notify Enabled'] === true || r['Notify Enabled'] === 1,
            isCompleted: r['Is Completed'] === 'true' || r['Is Completed'] === true || r['Is Completed'] === 1,
            currency: r.Currency || 'USD',
            createdAt: r['Created At'] || new Date().toISOString(),
            savingHistory: [],
        }));
    }

    // Parse Saving History sheet
    if (wb.SheetNames.includes('Saving History') || wb.SheetNames.includes('SavingHistory')) {
        const sheetName = wb.SheetNames.includes('Saving History') ? 'Saving History' : 'SavingHistory';
        const ws = wb.Sheets[sheetName];
        const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws);
        const historyByGoal = {};
        for (const r of rows) {
            const goalId = r['Goal Id'];
            if (!goalId) continue;
            if (!historyByGoal[goalId]) {
                historyByGoal[goalId] = [];
            }
            historyByGoal[goalId].push({
                id: r['Entry Id'] || generateId(),
                amount: parseFloat(r.Amount) || 0,
                date: r.Date ? new Date(r.Date).toISOString() : new Date().toISOString(),
                createdAt: r['Created At'] || new Date().toISOString(),
            });
        }

        // Link history back to saving goals
        if (result.savingsGoals) {
            for (const goal of result.savingsGoals) {
                if (historyByGoal[goal.id]) {
                    goal.savingHistory = historyByGoal[goal.id];
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Parse Transactions sheet
    if (wb.SheetNames.includes('Transactions')) {
        const ws = wb.Sheets['Transactions'];
        const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws);
        result.transactions = rows.map(r => {
            let parsedItems = [];
            if (r.Items) {
                try {
                    parsedItems = JSON.parse(r.Items);
                } catch (_) {
