================ FILE: services/exchangeRates.js ================

import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

const CACHE_KEY = 'exchangeRates';
const CACHE_DURATION_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 24 hours
const FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS = 8000; // 8 seconds

// Approximate baseline rates relative to 1 USD for offline first-launch fallback
// Prevents catastrophic 1:1 fallback (e.g. 1,000,000 IDR being treated as $1,000,000 USD)
const OFFLINE_USD_BASELINE_RATES = {
    USD: 1,
    IDR: 16200,
    MYR: 4.45,
    SGD: 1.34,
    EUR: 0.92,
    AUD: 1.52,
    GBP: 0.79,
    JPY: 154,
    CNY: 7.24,
    THB: 34.5,
    KRW: 1380,
    INR: 84.0,
    PHP: 58.0,
    VND: 25400,
    CAD: 1.39,
};

export async function fetchExchangeRates(baseCurrency = 'USD') {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS);

    try {
        const res = await fetch(`https://open.er-api.com/v6/latest/${baseCurrency}`, {
            signal: controller.signal,
        });
        if (!res.ok) return null;
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data && data.result === 'success' && data.rates) {
            const ratesData = {
                rates: data.rates,
                base: data.base_code || baseCurrency,
                timestamp: Date.now(),
            };
            await AsyncStorage.setItem(CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(ratesData));
            return ratesData;
        }
        return null;
    } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Failed to fetch exchange rates (offline or timeout):', e?.message || e);
        return null;
    } finally {
        clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    }
}

export async function getOrFetchRates(baseCurrency = 'USD') {
    let staleCache = null;
    try {
        const cached = await AsyncStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY);
        if (cached) {
            const data = JSON.parse(cached);
            if (data?.rates) {
                staleCache = data;
                if (data.base === baseCurrency && Date.now() - data.timestamp < CACHE_DURATION_MS) {
                    return data;
                }
            }
        }
        const fresh = await fetchExchangeRates(baseCurrency);
        if (fresh) return fresh;
        // If offline, return stale cache rather than null
        if (staleCache) return staleCache;
    } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Error getting rates:', e);
        if (staleCache) return staleCache;
    }
    return {
        base: 'USD',
        rates: OFFLINE_USD_BASELINE_RATES,
        timestamp: 0,
        isOfflineFallback: true,
    };
}

export function convertCurrency(amount, fromCurrency, toCurrency, exchangeRatesData) {
    const num = Number(amount);
    if (!num || !Number.isFinite(num)) return 0;
    if (!fromCurrency || !toCurrency || fromCurrency === toCurrency) return num;

    let rates = exchangeRatesData?.rates || exchangeRatesData;
    if (!rates || typeof rates !== 'object' || Object.keys(rates).length === 0) {
        rates = OFFLINE_USD_BASELINE_RATES;
    }

    const fromRate = Number(rates[fromCurrency]);
    const toRate = Number(rates[toCurrency]);

    if (!fromRate || !toRate || !Number.isFinite(fromRate) || !Number.isFinite(toRate) || fromRate <= 0 || toRate <= 0) {
        // Fall back to baseline USD rates if either rate is missing or non-positive
        const fallbackFrom = Number(OFFLINE_USD_BASELINE_RATES[fromCurrency]);
        const fallbackTo = Number(OFFLINE_USD_BASELINE_RATES[toCurrency]);
        if (fallbackFrom && fallbackTo && fallbackFrom > 0 && fallbackTo > 0) {
            return (num / fallbackFrom) * fallbackTo;
        }
        return num;
    }

    const amountInBase = num / fromRate;
    return amountInBase * toRate;
}


================ FILE: services/localAI.js ================

// On-Device AI Service using llama.rn (llama.cpp React Native binding)
// Manages local GGUF model lifecycle: download, init, completion

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import * as FileSystem from 'expo-file-system';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { initLlama } from 'llama.rn';
import { getModelConfig, LOCAL_MODELS, getAllModels } from './localAIConfig';

const ACTIVE_MODEL_KEY = '@financeflow_active_local_model';
const MODEL_CONTEXT_OVERRIDES_KEY = '@financeflow_model_context_overrides';
const DOWNLOAD_JOB_KEY = '@financeflow_model_download_job_v1';

// Qwen ChatML stop tokens
const STOP_WORDS = [
    '\x3c|im_end|\x3e',
    '\x3c|endoftext|\x3e',
    '\x3c/s\x3e',
];

// ─── State ────────────────────────────────────
let llamaContext = null;
let activeModelId = 'qwen3.5-0.8b'; // Default fallback
let modelStatus = 'idle'; // idle | downloading | paused | downloaded | loading | ready | error
let downloadProgress = 0;
let statusListeners = [];
let isCheckingStatus = false;
let modelContextOverrides = {};
let overridesLoaded = false;
let multimodalInitialized = false;
let completionQueue = Promise.resolve();
let activeCompletions = 0;
let pendingRelease = false;
let currentDownloadPromise = null;
let currentDownloadModelId = null;
let autoResumeChecked = false;
let llmResumable = null;
let visionResumable = null;
let lastDownloadCallback = null;
let pauseRequested = false;


function queueCompletion(taskFn) {
    activeCompletions += 1;
    const run = completionQueue.then(taskFn, taskFn);
    completionQueue = run.catch(() => undefined);
    return run.finally(() => {
        activeCompletions = Math.max(0, activeCompletions - 1);
        if (activeCompletions === 0 && pendingRelease) {
            pendingRelease = false;
            releaseModelInternal().catch(() => undefined);
        }
    });
}

async function loadDownloadJob() {
    try {
        const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(DOWNLOAD_JOB_KEY);
        return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

async function saveDownloadJob(job) {
    try {
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(DOWNLOAD_JOB_KEY, JSON.stringify(job));
    } catch {
        // Best effort persistence only.
    }
}

async function clearDownloadJob() {
    try {
        await AsyncStorage.removeItem(DOWNLOAD_JOB_KEY);
    } catch {
        // Best effort persistence only.
    }
}


async function maybeResumeDownloadJob() {
    if (autoResumeChecked || Platform.OS === 'web') return;
    autoResumeChecked = true;

    const pending = await loadDownloadJob();
    if (!pending?.modelId) return;

    const done = await isModelDownloaded(pending.modelId);
    if (done) {
        await clearDownloadJob();
        return;
    }

    modelStatus = 'downloading';
    downloadProgress = Number.isFinite(pending.lastProgress) ? pending.lastProgress : 0;
    notifyListeners();

    downloadModel(pending.modelId).catch((err) => {
        console.warn('Auto-resume model download failed:', err?.message || err);
    });
}

function clamp(value, min, max) {
    return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
}

async function ensureOverridesLoaded() {
    if (overridesLoaded) return;
    try {
        const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(MODEL_CONTEXT_OVERRIDES_KEY);
        modelContextOverrides = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
    } catch {
        modelContextOverrides = {};
    }
    overridesLoaded = true;
}

function resolveModelContextLength(config, id) {
    const min = config.minContextLength || 1024;
    const max = config.maxContextLength || config.contextLength || 4096;
    const override = modelContextOverrides[id];
    const candidate = Number.isFinite(override) ? override : config.contextLength;
    return clamp(candidate, min, max);
}

export function getContextPresets(config) {
    const min = config.minContextLength || 1024;
    const max = config.maxContextLength || config.contextLength || 4096;

    return [
        { label: 'Lowest', value: clamp(2048, min, max) },
        { label: 'Low', value: clamp(4096, min, max) },
        { label: 'Medium', value: clamp(8192, min, max) },
        { label: 'High', value: clamp(16384, min, max) },
        { label: 'Higher', value: clamp(24576, min, max) },
        { label: 'Maximum', value: clamp(32768, min, max) },
    ];
}

function describeLikelyCause(error, config, taskLabel) {
    const raw = String(error?.message || error || 'Unknown error');
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
    const task = taskLabel || 'request';

    if (lower.includes('out of memory') || lower.includes('oom') || lower.includes('malloc')) {
        return `${task} failed due to memory pressure (model too heavy for current RAM/settings).`;
    }

    if (lower.includes('mmproj') || lower.includes('multimodal') || lower.includes('vision')) {
        return `${task} failed because vision projector initialization failed for this model/build.`;
    }

    if (lower.includes('not downloaded')) {
        return `${task} failed because model files are missing or incomplete.`;
    }

    const modelName = config?.name || 'Active model';
    return `${task} failed while loading or running ${modelName}. Cause may be model/runtime compatibility on low-end hardware.`;
}

function normalizeModelId(id) {
    const found = getAllModels().find((m) => m.id === id);
    return found ? id : LOCAL_MODELS[0].id;
}

function notifyListeners() {
    const state = getModelState();
    statusListeners.forEach(fn => fn(state));
}

async function ensureMultimodalReady(config) {
    if (!config.visionFileName) {
        throw new Error('This model does not provide vision support files.');
    }

    if (!llamaContext) {
        throw new Error('Model context is not initialized.');
    }

    if (multimodalInitialized) {
        return true;
    }

    const visionPath = `${FileSystem.documentDirectory}models/${config.visionFileName}`;
    try {
        const success = await llamaContext.initMultimodal({
            path: visionPath,
            use_gpu: config.visionUseGpu === true,
        });

        if (!success) {
            throw new Error('Vision projector initialization returned false.');
        }

        multimodalInitialized = true;
        return true;
    } catch (visionError) {
        throw new Error(`Vision init failed for ${config.name}. Cause: ${visionError?.message || 'unknown native failure'}`);
    }
}

export function addStatusListener(fn) {
    statusListeners.push(fn);
    return () => {
        statusListeners = statusListeners.filter(l => l !== fn);
    };
}

export function getModelState() {
    const config = getModelConfig(activeModelId);
    return {
        status: modelStatus,
        downloadProgress,
        isReady: modelStatus === 'ready',
        isNative: Platform.OS !== 'web',
        modelName: config.name,
        modelSize: config.sizeLabel,
        activeModelId,
        contextLength: resolveModelContextLength(config, activeModelId),
        minContextLength: config.minContextLength || 1024,
        maxContextLength: config.maxContextLength || config.contextLength || 4096,
    };
}

export function getActiveModelConfig() {
    return getModelConfig(activeModelId);
}

export function activeModelSupportsVision() {
    const config = getModelConfig(activeModelId);
    return !!config.visionFileName;
}

export async function getActiveModelId() {
    try {
        const stored = await AsyncStorage.getItem(ACTIVE_MODEL_KEY);
        if (stored) {
            const normalized = normalizeModelId(stored);
            activeModelId = normalized;
            if (normalized !== stored) {
                await AsyncStorage.setItem(ACTIVE_MODEL_KEY, normalized);
            }
        }
    } catch { }
    await ensureOverridesLoaded();
    return activeModelId;
}

export async function getModelContextLength(id = activeModelId) {
    await ensureOverridesLoaded();
    const config = getModelConfig(id);
    return resolveModelContextLength(config, id);
}

export async function setModelContextLength(id, value) {
    await ensureOverridesLoaded();
    const config = getModelConfig(id);
    const min = config.minContextLength || 1024;
    const max = config.maxContextLength || config.contextLength || 4096;
    const normalized = clamp(parseInt(value, 10) || config.contextLength, min, max);

    modelContextOverrides[id] = normalized;
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(MODEL_CONTEXT_OVERRIDES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modelContextOverrides));

    if (id === activeModelId && llamaContext) {
        await releaseModel();
    }

    notifyListeners();
    return normalized;
}

export function validateActiveModelForTask(task) {
    const config = getModelConfig(activeModelId);
    if (task === 'chatbot' && config.recommendedForChat === false) {
        throw new Error('Model blocked for chatbot to prevent crash. Cause: this vision model is unstable for long chat sessions on low-end hardware. Use Qwen 3.5 0.8B for chatbot, or lower context to 1024.');
    }
    if (task === 'pdf-read' && config.recommendedForPdf === false) {
        throw new Error('Model blocked for PDF parsing. Cause: this model is not recommended for long OCR/document text parsing. Use LightOnOCR 1B, Qwen3 VL 2B IQ4_XS, Qwen2.5 VL 2B Q4, Moondream Vision, or Qwen 3.5 0.8B with OCR mode.');
    }
    return true;
}

export async function setActiveModelId(id) {
    activeModelId = normalizeModelId(id);
    try {
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(ACTIVE_MODEL_KEY, activeModelId);
    } catch { }
    // If ready, we trigger a release to force re-init next time
    if (llamaContext) await releaseModel();
    await checkModelStatus();
}

// ─── Model File Management ────────────────────
function getModelPath(id = activeModelId) {
    const config = getModelConfig(id);
    return `${FileSystem.documentDirectory}models/${config.fileName}`;
}

function getLegacyModelPaths(id = activeModelId) {
    const config = getModelConfig(id);
    return (config.legacyFileNames || []).map((fileName) => `${FileSystem.documentDirectory}models/${fileName}`);
}

async function cleanupLegacyModelFiles(id = activeModelId) {
    const legacyPaths = getLegacyModelPaths(id);
    for (const filePath of legacyPaths) {
        const info = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(filePath);
        if (info.exists) {
            await FileSystem.deleteAsync(filePath, { idempotent: true });
        }
    }
}

function toDirectDownloadUrl(url) {
    if (!url) return url;
    return url.includes('?') ? `${url}&download=true` : `${url}?download=true`;
}

export async function isModelDownloaded(id = activeModelId) {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') return false;
    const config = getModelConfig(id);
    const modelPath = getModelPath(id);
    try {
        const info = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(modelPath);
        if (!info.exists) return false;
        if (config.visionFileName) {
            const visionPath = `${FileSystem.documentDirectory}models/${config.visionFileName}`;
            const visionInfo = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(visionPath);
            if (!visionInfo.exists) return false;
        }
        return true;
    } catch {
        return false;
    }
}



export async function pauseDownloadModel() {
    if (modelStatus !== 'downloading') return;
    pauseRequested = true;
    modelStatus = 'paused';
    notifyListeners();
    try {
        if (llmResumable) await llmResumable.pauseAsync();
        if (visionResumable) await visionResumable.pauseAsync();
    } catch (e) { console.warn('Failed to pause properly:', e); }
}

export async function resumeDownloadModel(id, onProgress) {
    if (modelStatus !== 'paused' && modelStatus !== 'error') return;
    return downloadModel(id || activeModelId, onProgress || lastDownloadCallback);
}

export async function cancelDownloadModel(id) {
    pauseRequested = true;
    try {
        if (llmResumable) await llmResumable.pauseAsync();
        if (visionResumable) await visionResumable.pauseAsync();
    } catch (e) { }
    llmResumable = null;
    visionResumable = null;
    currentDownloadPromise = null;
    currentDownloadModelId = null;
    await clearDownloadJob();
    const config = getModelConfig(id || activeModelId);
    if (config) {
        const modelPath = `${FileSystem.documentDirectory}models/${config.fileName}`;
        const info = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(modelPath);
        if (info.exists) await FileSystem.deleteAsync(modelPath, { idempotent: true });

        if (config.visionFileName) {
            const visionPath = `${FileSystem.documentDirectory}models/${config.visionFileName}`;
            const vInfo = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(visionPath);
            if (vInfo.exists) await FileSystem.deleteAsync(visionPath, { idempotent: true });
        }
    }
    if ((id || activeModelId) === activeModelId) {
        modelStatus = 'idle';
        downloadProgress = 0;
        notifyListeners();
    }
}
export async function downloadModel(id, onProgress) {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
        throw new Error('On-device AI is not available on web. Use cloud AI instead.');
    }

    if (currentDownloadPromise && currentDownloadModelId === id && modelStatus === 'downloading') {
        return currentDownloadPromise;
    }

    pauseRequested = false;
    modelStatus = 'downloading';
    if (onProgress) lastDownloadCallback = onProgress;
    notifyListeners();

    const runDownload = async () => {
        let hasVision = false;
        let lastProgress = 0;
        let llmTotalBytes = 0;
        let llmWrittenBytes = 0;
        let visionTotalBytes = 0;
        let visionWrittenBytes = 0;
        try {
            const modelsDir = `${FileSystem.documentDirectory}models/`;
            const dirInfo = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(modelsDir);
            if (!dirInfo.exists) await FileSystem.makeDirectoryAsync(modelsDir, { intermediates: true });

            const config = getModelConfig(id);
            const modelPath = getModelPath(id);
            hasVision = !!config.visionUrl;
            const requestHeaders = { Accept: 'application/octet-stream', 'User-Agent': 'FinanceFlow/1.0' };

            await cleanupLegacyModelFiles(id);

            const savedJob = await loadDownloadJob();
            let job = savedJob?.modelId === id
                ? savedJob
                : {
                    modelId: id, phase: 'llm', lastProgress: 0,
                    llmTotalBytes: config.sizeBytes || 0, llmWrittenBytes: 0,
                    visionTotalBytes: config.visionSizeBytes || 0, visionWrittenBytes: 0,
                    llmSavable: null, visionSavable: null, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                };

            lastProgress = Number.isFinite(job.lastProgress) ? job.lastProgress : 0;
            llmTotalBytes = Number.isFinite(job.llmTotalBytes) && job.llmTotalBytes > 0 ? job.llmTotalBytes : (config.sizeBytes || 0);
            llmWrittenBytes = Math.max(0, Math.min(Number(job.llmWrittenBytes) || 0, llmTotalBytes || Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER));
            visionTotalBytes = Number.isFinite(job.visionTotalBytes) && job.visionTotalBytes > 0 ? job.visionTotalBytes : (config.visionSizeBytes || 0);
            visionWrittenBytes = Math.max(0, Math.min(Number(job.visionWrittenBytes) || 0, visionTotalBytes || Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER));

            if (lastProgress > 0) {
                downloadProgress = lastProgress;
                if (lastDownloadCallback) lastDownloadCallback(downloadProgress);
                notifyListeners();
            }

            let lastPersistTs = 0;
            const persistJob = async (extra = {}) => {
                const now = Date.now();
                if (!extra.force && now - lastPersistTs < 1000) return;
                job = { ...job, ...extra, modelId: id, llmTotalBytes, llmWrittenBytes, visionTotalBytes, visionWrittenBytes, lastProgress, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
                await saveDownloadJob(job);
                lastPersistTs = now;
            };

            const emitProgress = () => {
                const modelKnown = Math.max(1, Number(config.sizeBytes || llmTotalBytes || 1));
                const visionKnown = hasVision ? Math.max(1, Number(config.visionSizeBytes || visionTotalBytes || 1)) : 0;
                const totalBytes = modelKnown + visionKnown;
                const effectiveLlmWritten = Math.min(modelKnown, Math.max(llmWrittenBytes, 0));
                const effectiveVisionWritten = hasVision ? Math.min(visionKnown, Math.max(visionWrittenBytes, 0)) : 0;
                const writtenBytes = effectiveLlmWritten + effectiveVisionWritten;
                if (totalBytes <= 0) return;
                const pct = Math.min(100, Math.max(lastProgress, Math.round((writtenBytes / totalBytes) * 100)));
                if (pct === lastProgress) return;
                lastProgress = pct;
                downloadProgress = pct;
                if (lastDownloadCallback) lastDownloadCallback(pct);
                notifyListeners();
                persistJob();
            };

            const resolveResumeData = (value) => (value && typeof value !== 'string') ? value.resumeData : value;

            if (job.phase === 'llm') {
                const llmResumeData = resolveResumeData(job.llmSavable);
                llmResumable = FileSystem.createDownloadResumable(
                    toDirectDownloadUrl(config.downloadUrl), modelPath, { headers: requestHeaders },
                    async (progress) => {
                        if (pauseRequested) return;
                        if (progress.totalBytesExpectedToWrite > 0) {
                            llmTotalBytes = Math.max(config.sizeBytes || 0, progress.totalBytesExpectedToWrite);
                            llmWrittenBytes = Math.min(progress.totalBytesWritten, llmTotalBytes);
                            emitProgress();
                            if (llmResumable) await persistJob({ phase: 'llm', llmSavable: llmResumable.savable()?.resumeData || null });
                        }
                    }, llmResumeData
                );

                await persistJob({ phase: 'llm', llmSavable: llmResumable.savable()?.resumeData || null, force: true });

                let result = llmResumeData ? await llmResumable.resumeAsync() : await llmResumable.downloadAsync();
                if (pauseRequested) return;
                if (!result || !result.uri) throw new Error('LLM Download failed — no file URI returned');
                if (result.status && result.status >= 400) {
                    await FileSystem.deleteAsync(modelPath, { idempotent: true });
                    throw new Error(`LLM Download failed with HTTP ${result.status}.`);
                }

                const info = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(modelPath);
                if (info.exists && info.size < 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
                    await FileSystem.deleteAsync(modelPath);
                    throw new Error(`LLM Download failed. URL might be broken (Received ${info.size} bytes).`);
                }

                llmWrittenBytes = Math.max(llmWrittenBytes, info.size || llmTotalBytes);
                emitProgress();
                await persistJob({ phase: hasVision ? 'vision' : 'done', llmSavable: null, force: true });
            }

            if (hasVision && (job.phase === 'vision' || job.phase === 'llm')) {
                const visionPath = `${FileSystem.documentDirectory}models/${config.visionFileName}`;
                const visionResumeData = resolveResumeData(job.visionSavable);
                visionResumable = FileSystem.createDownloadResumable(
                    toDirectDownloadUrl(config.visionUrl), visionPath, { headers: requestHeaders },
                    (progress) => {
                        if (pauseRequested) return;
                        if (progress.totalBytesExpectedToWrite > 0) {
                            visionTotalBytes = Math.max(config.visionSizeBytes || 0, progress.totalBytesExpectedToWrite);
                            visionWrittenBytes = Math.min(progress.totalBytesWritten, visionTotalBytes);
                            emitProgress();
                            if (visionResumable) persistJob({ phase: 'vision', visionSavable: visionResumable.savable()?.resumeData || null });
                        }
                    }, visionResumeData
                );

                await persistJob({ phase: 'vision', visionSavable: visionResumable.savable()?.resumeData || null, force: true });

                let result = visionResumeData ? await visionResumable.resumeAsync() : await visionResumable.downloadAsync();
                if (pauseRequested) return;
                if (!result || !result.uri) throw new Error('Vision array Download failed');
                if (result.status && result.status >= 400) {
                    await FileSystem.deleteAsync(visionPath, { idempotent: true });
                    throw new Error(`Vision array Download failed with HTTP ${result.status}.`);
                }

                const visionInfo = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(visionPath);
                if (visionInfo.exists && visionInfo.size < 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
                    await FileSystem.deleteAsync(visionPath);
                    throw new Error(`Vision array Download failed (Received ${visionInfo.size} bytes).`);
                }

                visionWrittenBytes = Math.max(visionWrittenBytes, visionInfo.size || visionTotalBytes);
                emitProgress();
                await persistJob({ phase: 'done', visionSavable: null, force: true });
            }

            if (pauseRequested) return;

            await clearDownloadJob();
            llmResumable = null;
            visionResumable = null;

            if (id === activeModelId) {
                modelStatus = 'downloaded';
                downloadProgress = 100;
                notifyListeners();
            }
            return true;
        } catch (error) {
            if (pauseRequested) return;
            await saveDownloadJob({
                ...(await loadDownloadJob()),
                modelId: id, phase: hasVision ? 'vision' : 'llm',
                lastProgress, llmTotalBytes, llmWrittenBytes, visionTotalBytes, visionWrittenBytes,
                updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
            });

            if (id === activeModelId) {
                modelStatus = 'error';
                notifyListeners();
            }
            throw error;
        }
    };

    currentDownloadModelId = id;
    currentDownloadPromise = runDownload().finally(() => {
        if (!pauseRequested) {
            currentDownloadPromise = null;
            currentDownloadModelId = null;
        }
    });

    return currentDownloadPromise;
}

export async function deleteModel(id) {
    try {
        const config = getModelConfig(id);
        const modelPath = getModelPath(id);
        const info = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(modelPath);
        if (info.exists) {
            await FileSystem.deleteAsync(modelPath);
        }

        await cleanupLegacyModelFiles(id);

        if (config.visionFileName) {
            const visionPath = `${FileSystem.documentDirectory}models/${config.visionFileName}`;
            const stillUsedByOtherModel = getAllModels().some((m) => {
                if (m.id === id) return false;
                if (!m.visionFileName || m.visionFileName !== config.visionFileName) return false;
                return true;
            });

            if (!stillUsedByOtherModel) {
                const visionInfo = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(visionPath);
                if (visionInfo.exists) {
                    await FileSystem.deleteAsync(visionPath);
                }
            }
        }
        if (id === activeModelId) {
            modelStatus = 'idle';
            downloadProgress = 0;
            if (llamaContext) await releaseModel();
            else notifyListeners();
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to delete model:', error);
    }
}

// ─── Model Initialization ─────────────────────



export async function initModel() {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') return false;
    await getActiveModelId(); // ensure we have newest active selection
    if (llamaContext) return true; // Already loaded

    if (!(await isModelDownloaded(activeModelId))) {
        throw new Error('Model not downloaded. Please download it first from Settings.');
    }

    modelStatus = 'loading';
    notifyListeners();

    try {
        await ensureOverridesLoaded();
        const config = getModelConfig(activeModelId);
        const modelPath = getModelPath(activeModelId);
        const nCtx = resolveModelContextLength(config, activeModelId);

        const initOptions = {
            model: modelPath,
            use_mlock: false,
            n_ctx: nCtx,
            n_gpu_layers: Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 99 : (config.gpuLayers || 0),
            n_threads: Platform.OS === 'android' ? 4 : 6,
            flash_attn: true,
            ctx_shift: config.visionFileName ? false : true,
        };

        // If the model supports thinking, check the user's preference
        if (config.canThink) {
            let isThinkingEnabled = false;
            try {
                const rawPref = await AsyncStorage.getItem('@financeflow_ai_thinking_prefs_v1');
                const prefs = rawPref ? JSON.parse(rawPref) : {};
                isThinkingEnabled = !!prefs[activeModelId];
            } catch { }

            // If user explicitly disabled it, forcefully budget it to 0
            if (!isThinkingEnabled) {
                initOptions.reasoning_budget = 0;
            }
        }

        llamaContext = await initLlama(initOptions);
        multimodalInitialized = false;

        modelStatus = 'ready';
        notifyListeners();
        return true;
    } catch (error) {
        modelStatus = 'error';
        llamaContext = null;
        notifyListeners();
        console.error('Failed to init LlamaContext:', error);
        const config = getModelConfig(activeModelId);
        const cause = describeLikelyCause(error, config, 'Model initialization');
        throw new Error(`${cause} Raw error: ${error?.message || 'unknown'}`);
    }
}

export async function releaseModel() {
    if (activeCompletions > 0) {
        pendingRelease = true;
        try {
            await completionQueue;
        } catch {
            // Ignore completion errors while waiting to release.
        }
    }

    pendingRelease = false;
    await releaseModelInternal();
}

async function releaseModelInternal() {
    if (llamaContext) {
        try {
            await llamaContext.release();
        } catch { }
        llamaContext = null;
        multimodalInitialized = false;
    }
    modelStatus = 'idle';
    notifyListeners();
}

// ─── Chat Completion ──────────────────────────
export async function stopCurrentCompletion() {
    if (llamaContext && activeCompletions > 0) {
        try {
            await llamaContext.stopCompletion();
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn('Failed to stop local AI completion:', e);
        }
    }
}

export async function chatCompletion(messages, onToken, options = {}) {
    try {
        if (!llamaContext) {
            await initModel();
        }

        if (!llamaContext) {
            throw new Error('Local AI model is not loaded.');
        }

        const config = getModelConfig(activeModelId);
        const completionParams = {
            n_predict: options.maxTokens || 512,
            stop: STOP_WORDS,
            temperature: options.temperature || 0.7,
            top_p: options.topP || 0.8,
            top_k: options.topK || 20,
            repeat_penalty: options.repeatPenalty || 1.05,
        };

        if (config.visionFileName) {
            // Vision-language models (SmolVLM, Qwen2.5-VL, etc.) use messages array format.
            completionParams.messages = messages.map((m) => ({
                role: m.role,
                content: [{ type: 'text', text: m.content }],
            }));
        } else {
            // Qwen ChatML raw prompt format for text-only models.
            const rawPrompt = messages.map(m => `<|im_start|>${m.role}\n${m.content}<|im_end|>`).join('\n') + '\n<|im_start|>assistant\n';
            completionParams.prompt = rawPrompt;
        }

        const result = await queueCompletion(async () => llamaContext.completion(
            completionParams,
            (data) => {
                if (onToken && data.token) {
                    onToken(data.token);
                }
            }
        ));

        return result.text || '';
    } catch (error) {
        const config = getModelConfig(activeModelId);
        const cause = describeLikelyCause(error, config, 'Chatbot request');
        throw new Error(`${cause} Raw error: ${error?.message || 'unknown'}`);
    }
}

export async function textCompletion(prompt, imagePath = null, onToken, options = {}) {
    try {
        if (!llamaContext) {
            await initModel();
        }

        if (!llamaContext) {
            throw new Error('Local AI model is not loaded.');
        }

        // Format as ChatML and pre-close <think></think> when disableThinking is set
        // This stops Qwen 3.5 from wasting 400+ tokens on internal thinking before outputting JSON!
        const formattedPrompt = options.disableThinking
            ? `<|im_start|>system\nYou are a fast JSON extraction engine. Output valid JSON only.<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>user\n${prompt}<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n<think>\n</think>\n`
            : `<|im_start|>user\n${prompt}<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n`;

        const completionParams = {
            prompt: formattedPrompt,
            n_predict: options.maxTokens || 768,
            stop: [...STOP_WORDS, ...(options.stop || [])],
            temperature: options.temperature ?? 0.1,
            top_p: options.topP || 0.9,
            top_k: options.topK || 20,
        };

        if (imagePath) {
            const config = getModelConfig(activeModelId);
            if (config.visionFileName) {
                await ensureMultimodalReady(config);
            }

            completionParams.messages = [
                {
                    role: 'user',
                    content: [
                        { type: 'text', text: prompt },
                        { type: 'image_url', image_url: { url: imagePath } }
                    ]
                }
            ];
            delete completionParams.prompt;
        }

        const result = await queueCompletion(async () => llamaContext.completion(
            completionParams,
            (data) => {
                if (onToken && data.token) {
                    onToken(data.token);
                }
            }
        ));

        return result.text || '';
    } catch (error) {
        const config = getModelConfig(activeModelId);
        const cause = describeLikelyCause(error, config, 'Receipt parsing');
        throw new Error(`${cause} Raw error: ${error?.message || 'unknown'}`);
    }
}

// ─── Check status on startup ──────────────────
export async function checkModelStatus() {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web' || isCheckingStatus) return;
    isCheckingStatus = true;
    try {
        await maybeResumeDownloadJob();
        await getActiveModelId();
        await ensureOverridesLoaded();
        const downloaded = await isModelDownloaded(activeModelId);
        const pending = await loadDownloadJob();
        const hasActiveDownload = !!currentDownloadPromise && currentDownloadModelId === activeModelId;

        if (downloaded && pending?.modelId === activeModelId) {
            await clearDownloadJob();
        }

        if (hasActiveDownload) {
            modelStatus = 'downloading';
        } else if (llamaContext) {
            modelStatus = 'ready';
        } else if (pending && pending.modelId === activeModelId) {
            modelStatus = 'paused';
            downloadProgress = pending.lastProgress || 0;
        } else {
            modelStatus = downloaded ? 'downloaded' : 'idle';
        }

        if (downloaded && !hasActiveDownload) {
            downloadProgress = 100;
        }
        notifyListeners();
    } finally {
        isCheckingStatus = false;
    }
}

// ─── Utility ──────────────────────────────────
export function isLocalAIAvailable() {
    return Platform.OS !== 'web' && modelStatus === 'ready';
}

export function isLocalAIDownloaded() {
    return modelStatus === 'downloaded' || modelStatus === 'ready' || modelStatus === 'loading';
}

// ─── Custom Model Persistence ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

const CUSTOM_MODELS_KEY = '@financeflow_custom_models_v1';

let _cachedCustomModels = null;

export async function loadCustomModels() {
    try {
        const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(CUSTOM_MODELS_KEY);
        _cachedCustomModels = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
    } catch {
        _cachedCustomModels = [];
    }
    return _cachedCustomModels;
}

export function getCachedCustomModels() {
    return _cachedCustomModels || [];
}

export async function saveCustomModel(model) {
    const models = await loadCustomModels();
    const exists = models.findIndex(m => m.id === model.id);
    if (exists >= 0) {
        models[exists] = { ...models[exists], ...model };
    } else {
        models.push({ ...model, isCustom: true });
    }
    _cachedCustomModels = models;
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(CUSTOM_MODELS_KEY, JSON.stringify(models));
    return models;
}

export async function removeCustomModel(id) {
    let models = await loadCustomModels();
    models = models.filter(m => m.id !== id);
    _cachedCustomModels = models;
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(CUSTOM_MODELS_KEY, JSON.stringify(models));
    return models;
}

export function getAllModels() {
    return [...LOCAL_MODELS, ...getCachedCustomModels()];
}

export function getModelConfig(id) {
    return getAllModels().find(m => m.id === id) || LOCAL_MODELS[0];
}


================ FILE: services/localOCR.js ================

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
// import TextRecognition from '@react-native-ml-kit/text-recognition';
const TextRecognition = null;
import { extractPdfTextChunks, extractImageText, getReadableFileUri, isPdfDocumentUri } from './documentText';
import { LOCAL_MODELS } from './localAIConfig';

function normalizeOcrResult(raw) {
    if (!raw) return '';

    if (Array.isArray(raw)) {
        return raw.map(normalizeOcrResult).filter(Boolean).join('\n');
    }

    if (typeof raw === 'string') {
        return raw;
    }

    if (typeof raw === 'object') {
        const preferred = [raw.text, raw.value, raw.label, raw.content, raw.line, raw.block, raw.word].filter(Boolean);
        if (preferred.length > 0) {
            return preferred.join('\n');
        }
        return Object.values(raw).map(normalizeOcrResult).filter(Boolean).join('\n');
    }

    return '';
}

function cleanOcrText(text) {
    return String(text || '')
        .replace(/\r/g, '\n')
        .replace(/[ \t]+/g, ' ')
        .replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n')
        .trim();
}

async function extractTextWithMlKit(imageUri) {
    if (!TextRecognition?.recognize) {
        throw new Error('ML Kit text recognition is unavailable in this build.');
    }

    const readableUri = imageUri && imageUri.startsWith('content://')
        ? await getReadableFileUri(imageUri, 'financeflow_image_', '.jpg')
        : imageUri;
    const result = await Promise.race([
        TextRecognition.recognize(readableUri),
        new Promise((_, reject) =>
            setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Text recognition timed out.')), 25000)
        ),
    ]);
    return cleanOcrText(normalizeOcrResult(result?.text || result));
}

export async function extractTextFromImageWithMlKit(imageUri) {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
        throw new Error('Local OCR is not available on web.');
    }

    if (!imageUri) {
        throw new Error('Image URI is required for OCR.');
    }

    return extractTextWithMlKit(imageUri);
}

async function transcribeImageWithVisionModel(imageUri) {
    const {
        textCompletion,
        getActiveModelConfig,
        getActiveModelId,
        setActiveModelId,
        isModelDownloaded,
    } = require('./localAI');
    const model = getActiveModelConfig();

    if (!model?.visionFileName) {
        const previousModelId = await getActiveModelId();
        const candidateVisionModels = LOCAL_MODELS.filter((candidate) => !!candidate?.visionFileName);
        let selectedVisionModel = null;

        for (const candidate of candidateVisionModels) {
            try {
                if (await isModelDownloaded(candidate.id)) {
                    selectedVisionModel = candidate;
                    break;
                }
            } catch {
                // Keep checking other vision models.
            }
        }

        if (selectedVisionModel) {
            await setActiveModelId(selectedVisionModel.id);
            try {
                return await transcribeImageWithVisionModel(imageUri);
            } finally {
                await setActiveModelId(previousModelId);
            }
        }

        try {
            return await extractImageText(imageUri);
        } catch (ocrErr) {
            throw new Error(`Vision mode unavailable and OCR fallback failed. Use OCR requires either a downloaded vision model (LightOnOCR, Qwen3 VL, Qwen2.5 VL, Moondream Vision) or native text recognition support. Details: ${ocrErr?.message}`);
        }
    }

    const prompt = `Read this receipt image and transcribe all visible text exactly as plain lines.
Return plain text only.
Do not summarize.
Do not return JSON.`;

    const response = await textCompletion(prompt, imageUri, null, {
        maxTokens: 1200,
        temperature: 0,
    });

    const cleaned = cleanOcrText(normalizeOcrResult(response));
    if (!cleaned) {
        throw new Error('No readable text detected in image. Use a clearer receipt photo.');
    }

    return cleaned;
}

export async function extractTextFromImage(imageUri) {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
        throw new Error('Local OCR is not available on web.');
    }

    if (!imageUri) {
        throw new Error('Image URI is required for OCR.');
    }

    if (isPdfDocumentUri(imageUri)) {
        const extracted = await extractPdfTextChunks(imageUri, { maxPagesPerChunk: 2 });
        const cleaned = cleanOcrText(normalizeOcrResult(extracted.text));
        if (!cleaned) {
            throw new Error('No readable text detected in PDF/document.');
        }
        return cleaned;
    }

    try {
        const mlKitText = await extractTextWithMlKit(imageUri);
        if (mlKitText.length >= 12) {
            return mlKitText;
        }
    } catch {
        // Fall through to alternate OCR engines.
    }

    try {
        const nativeOcrText = cleanOcrText(normalizeOcrResult(await extractImageText(imageUri)));
        if (nativeOcrText.length >= 12) {
            return nativeOcrText;
        }
    } catch {
        // Fall through to vision-based transcription.
    }

    return transcribeImageWithVisionModel(imageUri);
}

// Best-effort OCR for generic document/image URI (including scanned-PDF fallback where supported).
export async function extractTextFromDocumentUri(uri) {
    if (!uri) {
        throw new Error('Document URI is required.');
    }

    try {
        if (isPdfDocumentUri(uri)) {
            const extracted = await extractPdfTextChunks(uri, { maxPagesPerChunk: 2 });
            const cleaned = cleanOcrText(normalizeOcrResult(extracted.text));
            if (!cleaned) {
                throw new Error('No readable text detected in PDF/document.');
            }
            return cleaned;
        }

        const mlKitText = await extractTextWithMlKit(uri);
        if (mlKitText.length >= 12) {
            return mlKitText;
        }

        const nativeOcrText = cleanOcrText(normalizeOcrResult(await extractImageText(uri)));
        if (nativeOcrText.length >= 12) {
            return nativeOcrText;
        }

        return nativeOcrText;
    } catch {
        return extractTextFromImage(uri);
    }
}


================ FILE: services/notificationService.js ================

/**
 * notificationService.js
 *
 * Handles all local push notification scheduling for savings reminders.
 * Uses expo-notifications to schedule repeating local notifications.
 */
import * as Notifications from 'expo-notifications';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';

const REMINDER_TIME_KEY = 'savings_reminder_time'; // stored as "HH:MM" (24h)
const DEFAULT_REMINDER_TIME = '09:00';

// Configure how notifications are shown when app is in foreground
Notifications.setNotificationHandler({
    handleNotification: async () => ({
        shouldShowAlert: true,
        shouldPlaySound: true,
        shouldSetBadge: false,
    }),
});

/**
 * Returns the stored reminder time as { hour, minute }, defaulting to 9:00 AM.
 */
export async function getReminderTime() {
    try {
        const stored = await AsyncStorage.getItem(REMINDER_TIME_KEY);
        const raw = stored || DEFAULT_REMINDER_TIME;
        const [h, m] = raw.split(':').map(Number);
        return { hour: isNaN(h) ? 9 : h, minute: isNaN(m) ? 0 : m, raw: stored || DEFAULT_REMINDER_TIME };
    } catch {
        return { hour: 9, minute: 0, raw: DEFAULT_REMINDER_TIME };
    }
}

/**
 * Persists the reminder time.
 * @param {string} timeStr - "HH:MM" format, e.g. "09:00"
 */
export async function saveReminderTime(timeStr) {
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(REMINDER_TIME_KEY, timeStr);
}

/**
 * Requests push notification permission from the OS.
 * Returns true if granted, false otherwise.
 */
export async function requestPermission() {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') return false;
    const { status: existingStatus } = await Notifications.getPermissionsAsync();
    if (existingStatus === 'granted') return true;
    const { status } = await Notifications.requestPermissionsAsync({
        ios: {
            allowAlert: true,
            allowBadge: false,
            allowSound: true,
        },
    });
    return status === 'granted';
}

/**
 * Builds the notification identifier for a goal.
 * Keeping a stable ID per goal lets us cancel it precisely.
 */
function notifId(goalId) {
    return `saving_reminder_${goalId}`;
}

/**
 * Schedules a repeating local notification for a savings goal.
 *
 * @param {object} goal - The savings goal object (must have id, title, commitment)
 * @param {number} hour - Hour to fire (0-23)
 * @param {number} minute - Minute to fire (0-59)
 */
export async function scheduleGoalReminder(goal, hour, minute) {
    if (!goal || !goal.notifyEnabled || !goal.commitment) return;

    // Cancel any existing notification for this goal before scheduling a new one
    await cancelGoalReminder(goal.id);

    const identifier = notifId(goal.id);

    let trigger;
    if (goal.commitment === 'daily') {
        trigger = {
            type: Notifications.SchedulableTriggerInputTypes.DAILY,
            hour,
            minute,
        };
    } else if (goal.commitment === 'weekly') {
        trigger = {
            type: Notifications.SchedulableTriggerInputTypes.WEEKLY,
            weekday: 2, // Monday (1=Sun, 2=Mon ... 7=Sat)
            hour,
            minute,
        };
    } else {
        // monthly — fire on the 1st of every month
        trigger = {
            type: Notifications.SchedulableTriggerInputTypes.MONTHLY,
            day: 1,
            hour,
            minute,
        };
    }

    const frequencyLabel = {
        daily: 'daily',
        weekly: 'every week',
        monthly: 'this month',
    }[goal.commitment] || 'regularly';

    try {
        await Notifications.scheduleNotificationAsync({
            identifier,
            content: {
                title: `💰 Savings Reminder — ${goal.title}`,
                body: `Don't forget to add your ${frequencyLabel} savings deposit to stay on track!`,
                data: { goalId: goal.id, type: 'savings_reminder' },
            },
            trigger,
        });
    } catch (err) {
        console.warn('[notificationService] Failed to schedule reminder:', err);
    }
}

/**
 * Cancels a previously scheduled notification for a savings goal.
 * @param {string} goalId
 */
export async function cancelGoalReminder(goalId) {
    try {
        await Notifications.cancelScheduledNotificationAsync(notifId(goalId));
    } catch {
        // Silently ignore — notification may not exist yet
    }
}

/**
 * Re-schedules ALL active goal reminders (e.g., when user changes global reminder time).
 *
 * @param {Array} goals - All savings goals from state
 * @param {number} hour
 * @param {number} minute
 */
export async function rescheduleAllReminders(goals, hour, minute) {
    const activeGoals = (goals || []).filter(g => g.notifyEnabled && g.commitment && !g.isCompleted);
    for (const goal of activeGoals) {
        await scheduleGoalReminder(goal, hour, minute);
    }
}

/**
 * Fires an immediate local notification for a recurring transaction that is due
 * and requires user confirmation before posting.
 *
 * @param {object} rt - The recurring transaction template object.
 *   rt.id, rt.templateData.description, rt.templateData.amount, rt.frequency
 */
export async function scheduleRecurringConfirmNotification(rt) {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') return;

    const { frequencyLabel } = require('./recurringEngine');
    const template = rt.templateData || {};
    const desc = template.description || 'Recurring Transaction';
    const amt = template.amount ? `$${Number(template.amount).toFixed(2)}` : '';
    const freqLabel = frequencyLabel(rt.frequency);

    try {
        await Notifications.scheduleNotificationAsync({
            identifier: `recurring_confirm_${rt.id}_${Date.now()}`,
            content: {
                title: `🔁 ${freqLabel} payment due`,
                body: `${desc}${amt ? ' · ' + amt : ''} — tap to review and post`,
                data: {
                    type: 'recurring_confirm',
                    recurringId: rt.id,
                    prefill: JSON.stringify(template),
                },
            },
            trigger: null, // fire immediately
        });
    } catch (err) {
        console.warn('[notificationService] Failed to schedule recurring confirm notification:', err);
    }
}


================ FILE: services/ocrService.js ================

// OCR Service - Cross-platform on-device text recognition
// iOS: Apple Vision framework (VNRecognizeTextRequest)
// Android: Google ML Kit Text Recognition
//
// Inspired by: https://github.com/AnastasiiaDesiateryk/receiptMobileApp-react-native-ocr

import { Platform, NativeModules } from 'react-native';
import TextRecognition from '@react-native-ml-kit/text-recognition';

// ─── Native module access ─────────────────────
function getOCRModule() {
    try {
        const { requireNativeModule } = require('expo-modules-core');
        const mod = requireNativeModule('ReceiptOCRModule');
        if (mod && mod.recognizeText) {
            return mod;
        }
    } catch (e) { }
    return null;
}

function getMLKitOCRModule() {
    if (!TextRecognition?.recognize) {
        return null;
    }
    return TextRecognition;
}

// ─── Path normalization ───────────────────────
function normalizePath(uri) {
    if (!uri) return uri;
    return uri.startsWith('file://') ? uri.replace('file://', '') : uri;
}

function normalizeFrame(frame, width, height) {
    if (!frame) {
        return { x: 0, y: 0, w: 0, h: 0 };
    }

    const imageWidth = width || Math.max(1, frame.left + frame.width);
    const imageHeight = height || Math.max(1, frame.top + frame.height);

    return {
        x: frame.left / imageWidth,
        y: frame.top / imageHeight,
        w: frame.width / imageWidth,
        h: frame.height / imageHeight,
        pixelX: Math.round(frame.left),
        pixelY: Math.round(frame.top),
        pixelW: Math.round(frame.width),
        pixelH: Math.round(frame.height),
    };
}

// ─── Geometric Line Reconstructor ───────────────
function detectClusterSlope(items) {
    const slopes = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        for (let j = 0; j < items.length; j++) {
            if (i === j) continue;
            const dx = items[j].cx - items[i].cx;
            const dy = items[j].cy - items[i].cy;
            if (dx > 0.30 && Math.abs(dy) < 0.045) {
                const s = dy / dx;
                if (Math.abs(s) <= 0.07) slopes.push(s);
            }
        }
    }
    if (slopes.length < 3) return 0;

    const zeroCount = slopes.filter(v => Math.abs(v) <= 0.010).length;
    let bestCenter = 0;
    let maxCount = 0;
    let bestSlopes = [];

    for (let s = -0.06; s <= 0.0601; s += 0.002) {
        const inWindow = slopes.filter(v => Math.abs(v - s) <= 0.010);
        if (inWindow.length > maxCount) {
            maxCount = inWindow.length;
            bestCenter = s;
            bestSlopes = inWindow;
        }
    }

    if (maxCount >= 3 && maxCount > zeroCount * 1.2 && Math.abs(bestCenter) >= 0.012) {
        return bestSlopes.reduce((sum, v) => sum + v, 0) / bestSlopes.length;
    }
    return 0;
}

function reconstructFullTextFromBlocks(blocks) {
    if (!blocks || blocks.length === 0) return '';
    const items = blocks.map(b => ({
        text: b.text.trim(),
        x1: b.bbox.x,
        x2: b.bbox.x + b.bbox.w,
        w: b.bbox.w,
        cx: b.bbox.x + (b.bbox.w / 2),
        cy: b.bbox.y + (b.bbox.h / 2),
        h: b.bbox.h
    })).filter(i => i.text.length > 0);

    const slope = detectClusterSlope(items);

    for (const it of items) {
        it.adjCy = it.cy - (slope * it.cx);
    }
    items.sort((a, b) => a.adjCy - b.adjCy);

    const rows = [];
    let currentRow = [];
    const threshFactor = Math.abs(slope) >= 0.012 ? 0.55 : 0.45;

    for (const item of items) {
        if (currentRow.length === 0) {
            currentRow.push(item);
        } else {
            const rowCy = currentRow[0].adjCy;
            const threshold = Math.max(currentRow[0].h, item.h) * threshFactor;
            const overlapsHorizontally = currentRow.some(existing => {
                const overlapX = Math.min(existing.x2, item.x2) - Math.max(existing.x1, item.x1);
                const minW = Math.min(existing.w, item.w);
                return overlapX > minW * 0.25;
            });

            if (Math.abs(item.adjCy - rowCy) <= threshold && !overlapsHorizontally) {
                currentRow.push(item);
            } else {
                rows.push(currentRow);
                currentRow = [item];
            }
        }
    }
    if (currentRow.length > 0) rows.push(currentRow);

    const lines = rows.map(row => {
        row.sort((a, b) => a.cx - b.cx);
        return row.map(i => i.text).join(' ');
    });

    let reconstructed = lines.join('\n');
    reconstructed = reconstructed.replace(/(rp|rm|sgd|hkd|\$)\s*(\d{1,2})\s+(\d{2,3}[.,]\d{3})\b/gi, '$1 $2$3');
    return reconstructed;
}

function normalizeMLKitResult(result) {
    const lines = [];
    let width = 0;
    let height = 0;

    for (const block of result?.blocks || []) {
        const blockLines = block?.lines?.length ? block.lines : [{ text: block?.text, frame: block?.frame }];

        for (const line of blockLines) {
            const frame = line?.frame || block?.frame;
            if (frame) {
                width = Math.max(width, frame.left + frame.width);
                height = Math.max(height, frame.top + frame.height);
            }
            if (line?.text) {
                lines.push({ text: line.text, frame });
            }
        }
    }

    // Sort blocks top-to-bottom (by vertical center) to restore reading order.
    // ML Kit returns blocks in arbitrary order; sorting by frame.top gives reliable ordering.
    lines.sort((a, b) => {
        const aY = (a.frame?.top ?? 0) + (a.frame?.height ?? 0) / 2;
        const bY = (b.frame?.top ?? 0) + (b.frame?.height ?? 0) / 2;
        return aY - bY;
    });

    const blocks = lines.map((line) => ({
        text: line.text,
        confidence: 1,
        bbox: normalizeFrame(line.frame, width, height),
    }));

    const reconstructedText = reconstructFullTextFromBlocks(blocks);

    return {
        fullText: reconstructedText || result?.text || lines.map((line) => line.text).join('\n'),
        blocks,
        width,
        height,
    };
}

// ─── Run on-device OCR ────────────────────────
export async function runOCR(imageUri) {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
        throw new Error('On-device OCR is not available on web.');
    }

    if (!imageUri) {
        throw new Error('Image URI is required for OCR.');
    }

    const mod = getOCRModule();
    if (mod) {
        const path = Platform.OS === 'ios' ? normalizePath(imageUri) : imageUri;
        const result = await mod.recognizeText(path);

        const blocks = (result?.blocks || []).map((block) => ({
            text: block.text || '',
            confidence: block.confidence ?? 1,
            bbox: block.bbox || { x: 0, y: 0, w: 0, h: 0 },
        }));

        const reconstructedText = reconstructFullTextFromBlocks(blocks);

        return {
            fullText: reconstructedText || result?.fullText || '',
            blocks,
            width: result?.width || 0,
            height: result?.height || 0,
        };
    }

    const mlKit = getMLKitOCRModule();
    if (!mlKit) {
        throw new Error(
            'On-device OCR is not available. Rebuild the app with:\n' +
            '  iOS: npx expo run:ios\n' +
            '  Android: npx expo run:android'
        );
    }

    const result = await mlKit.recognize(imageUri);
    return normalizeMLKitResult(result);
}

// ─── Check OCR availability ───────────────────
export function isOCRAvailable() {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') return false;
    return !!getOCRModule() || !!getMLKitOCRModule();
}

const MONTH_MAP = {
    jan: '01', feb: '02', mar: '03', apr: '04', mei: '05', may: '05',
    jun: '06', jul: '07', agt: '08', aug: '08', sep: '09', okt: '10',
    oct: '10', nov: '11', des: '12', dec: '12',
    januari: '01', februari: '02', maret: '03', april: '04', juni: '06',
    juli: '07', agustus: '08', september: '09', oktober: '10', november: '11', desember: '12',
    january: '01', february: '02', march: '03', june: '06', july: '07', august: '08', october: '10', december: '12',
};

function normLine(str) {
    return String(str || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
}

function parseMoney(text) {
    if (!text) return null;
    let s = text.trim();
    s = s.replace(/^[-\s]*(rm|rp|idr|usd|aud|sgd|myr|\$|\u00a3|\u20ac|\u00a5)\.?\s*/i, '');
    s = s.replace(/\s*(rm|rp|idr|usd|aud|sgd|myr|\$)\s*$/i, '');
    s = s.replace(/[^\d.,]/g, '');
    if (!s) return null;

    const commaCount = (s.match(/,/g) || []).length;
    const dotCount = (s.match(/\./g) || []).length;

    if (commaCount >= 1 && dotCount === 0) {
        const parts = s.split(',');
        if (parts.length > 2) return parseFloat(parts.join(''));
        if (parts[1].length === 3) return parseFloat(parts.join(''));
        if (parts[1].length === 2 || parts[1].length === 1) return parseFloat(`${parts[0]}.${parts[1]}`);
        return parseFloat(parts.join(''));
    }

    if (dotCount >= 1 && commaCount === 0) {
        const parts = s.split('.');
        if (parts.length > 2) return parseFloat(parts.join(''));
        if (parts[1].length === 3) return parseFloat(parts.join(''));
        if (parts[1].length === 2 || parts[1].length === 1) return parseFloat(`${parts[0]}.${parts[1]}`);
        return parseFloat(parts.join(''));
    }

    if (commaCount >= 1 && dotCount >= 1) {
        const lastComma = s.lastIndexOf(',');
        const lastDot = s.lastIndexOf('.');
        if (lastDot > lastComma) {
            return parseFloat(s.replace(/,/g, ''));
        }
        return parseFloat(s.replace(/\./g, '').replace(',', '.'));
    }

    return parseFloat(s);
}

function getMoneyCandidates(line) {
    const text = String(line || '');
    const candidates = [];
    const moneyPattern = /(?:(?:rp|rm|sgd|hkd|\$)\s*)?((?:\d{1,3}(?:[.,]\d{3})+(?:[.,]\d{1,2})?|\d+(?:[.,]\d{1,2})?))/gi;
    let match;

    while ((match = moneyPattern.exec(text)) !== null) {
        const raw = match[0];
        const leadingSpaces = raw.match(/^\s*/)?.[0]?.length || 0;
        const start = match.index + leadingSpaces;
        const end = match.index + raw.length;
        const before = text[start - 1] || '';
        const after = text[end] || '';
        const afterText = text.slice(end, end + 3);
        const beforeText = text.slice(Math.max(0, start - 3), start);
        const hasCurrency = /(?:rp|idr|usd|aud|sgd|myr|rm|hkd|\$)/i.test(raw);
        const hasMoneySeparator = /[.,]\d{3}\b/.test(raw) || /\d+[.,]\d{1,2}\b/.test(raw);

        if (/[a-z]/i.test(before) || /[a-z]/i.test(after)) continue;
        if (before === '-' && after === '-') continue;
        if (/^\s*%/.test(afterText)) continue;

        if (/(?:^|[^a-z])[xX]\s*$|@\s*$/i.test(beforeText)) {
            const remainingText = text.slice(end);
            if (/(?:rp|idr|usd|aud|sgd|myr|rm|hkd|\$)?\.?\s*\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?/i.test(remainingText)) {
                continue;
            }
        }

        const value = parseMoney(raw);
        if (value == null || value <= 0) continue;

        const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');

        if (!hasCurrency && !hasMoneySeparator && !/[.,]\d/.test(raw)) {
            const isDirectlyAtOrX = /[@xX]\s*$/i.test(beforeText);
            if (!isDirectlyAtOrX && value < 10) continue;
            if (value < 100 && !isDirectlyAtOrX) continue;
        }

        if (!hasCurrency && !hasMoneySeparator && digits.length === 4
            && value >= 1900 && value <= 2099) continue;

        if (!hasCurrency && !hasMoneySeparator && digits.length > 7) continue;

        candidates.push({
            value,
            raw,
            start,
            end,
            score: (hasCurrency ? 3 : 0) + (hasMoneySeparator ? 2 : 0) + Math.min(3, Math.floor(digits.length / 3)),
        });
    }

    return candidates;
}

function getBestMoneyValue(line) {
    const candidates = getMoneyCandidates(line);
    if (!candidates.length) return null;
    return candidates
        .slice()
        .sort((a, b) => (b.start - a.start) || (b.score - a.score) || (b.value - a.value))[0].value;
}

function almostSameAmount(a, b) {
    return Math.abs(a - b) <= Math.max(0.01, Math.max(Math.abs(a), Math.abs(b)) * 0.002);
}

function uniqueAmounts(values) {
    return values.reduce((unique, value) => (
        unique.some(existing => almostSameAmount(existing, value)) ? unique : [...unique, value]
    ), []);
}

function pickBestTotal(totalValues, expectedTotal) {
    if (!totalValues.length) return 0;
    if (expectedTotal > 0) {
        const tolerance = Math.max(1, expectedTotal * 0.03);
        const closest = totalValues
            .map(value => ({ value, diff: Math.abs(value - expectedTotal) }))
            .sort((a, b) => a.diff - b.diff)[0];
        if (closest.diff <= tolerance) return closest.value;
    }
    return Math.max(...totalValues);
}

function getLabeledMoneyValue(lines, index) {
    const sameLine = getBestMoneyValue(lines[index]);
    if (sameLine != null) return sameLine;

    for (let offset = 1; offset <= 3 && index + offset < lines.length; offset++) {
        const next = lines[index + offset];
        const lower = next.toLowerCase();

        if (/(tanggal|date|time|waktu|invoice|receipt|struk|bill|order|kasir|cashier|table|meja)/i.test(lower)) break;
        if (/(subtotal|sub total|sub-total|grand\s*total|total|amount|jumlah|tax|pajak|ppn|pb1|vat|gst|service|serv|svc|sc\b)/i.test(lower)) break;

        const nextValue = getBestMoneyValue(next);
        if (nextValue != null) return nextValue;
    }

    return null;
}

function parseDate(text) {
    const raw = normLine(text);
    if (!raw) return null;

    let stripped = raw
        .replace(/^(?:tanggal|tgl\.?\/jam|tgl|date\s*(?:[&]\s*time|and\s*time)?|date|waktu|time)\s*[:.|\-]?\s*/i, '')
        .trim();
    stripped = stripped.replace(/^(senin|selasa|rabu|kamis|jumat|sabtu|minggu|monday|tuesday|wednesday|thursday|friday|saturday|sunday)\s*[,.]?\s*/i, '').trim();
    stripped = stripped.replace(/['’]/g, ' ');

    const ymd = stripped.match(/(?:^|\D)(20\d{2}|19\d{2})[\/\.\-](\d{1,2})[\/\.\-](\d{1,2})(?:\D|$)/);
    if (ymd) return `${ymd[1]}-${ymd[2].padStart(2, '0')}-${ymd[3].padStart(2, '0')}`;

    const named = stripped.match(/(\d{1,2})[\s\/\.\-]+([a-z]+)[\s\/\.\-]*(\d{2,4})?/i);
    if (named) {
        const mm = MONTH_MAP[named[2].toLowerCase()];
        if (mm) {
            const yr = named[3] ? (named[3].length <= 2 ? `20${named[3]}` : named[3]) : '2026';
            return `${yr}-${mm}-${named[1].padStart(2, '0')}`;
        }
    }

    const named2 = stripped.match(/([a-z]+)[\s\/\.\-]+(\d{1,2})[,\s\/\.\-]*(\d{2,4})?/i);
    if (named2) {
        const mm = MONTH_MAP[named2[1].toLowerCase()];
        if (mm) {
            const yr = named2[3] ? (named2[3].length <= 2 ? `20${named2[3]}` : named2[3]) : '2026';
            return `${yr}-${mm}-${named2[2].padStart(2, '0')}`;
        }
    }

    const numDate = stripped.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{2,4})(?:\D|$)/);
    if (numDate) {
        const p1 = parseInt(numDate[1], 10);
        const p2 = parseInt(numDate[2], 10);
        let yyyy = numDate[3];
        if (yyyy.length === 2) yyyy = `20${yyyy}`;
        const yNum = parseInt(yyyy, 10);
        if (yNum >= 1990 && yNum <= 2099) {
            let dd, mm;
            if (p2 > 12 && p1 <= 12) {
                mm = String(p1).padStart(2, '0');
                dd = String(p2).padStart(2, '0');
            } else {
                dd = String(p1).padStart(2, '0');
                mm = String(p2).padStart(2, '0');
            }
            return `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}`;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

function inferCategory(itemName) {
    const lower = String(itemName || '').toLowerCase();
    if (/\b(nasi|rice|mie|noodle|ayam|chicken|beef|sapi|ikan|fish|udang|shrimp|telur|egg|tahu|tempe|sayur|salad|soup|soto|bakso|sate|satay|pizza|burger|sandwich|roti|bread|cake|donut|ice cream|es krim|kopi|coffee|tea|teh|juice|jus|soda|coca|pepsi|fanta|sprite|bir|beer|wine|water|air|mineral|susu|milk|makan|drink|minuman|makanan|dessert|appetizer|snack|fries|pasta|steak|lobster|crab|lamb|pork|babi|dimsum|sushi|ramen|curry|rendang|gado|pecel|rawon|gudeg|martabak|gorengan|kerupuk|sambal|latte|cappuccino|matcha|crepes|croissant|gelato)\b/.test(lower)) return 'food';
    if (/\b(sabun|soap|shampo|shampoo|tissue|tisu|detergent|deterjen|sikat|tooth|pasta|gigi|diapers|popok|sanitary|pembalut|cotton|kapas|hand sanitizer|masker|mask|vitamin|obat|medicine|gel|lotion|deodorant|parfum|perfume)\b/.test(lower)) return 'daily_needs';
    if (/\b(bensin|gasoline|fuel|solar|diesel|parkir|parking|tol|toll|grab|gojek|uber|taxi|taksi|ojek|angkot|bus|kereta|train|tiket|ticket|transport)\b/.test(lower)) return 'transport';
    if (/\b(konsultasi|consultation|doctor|dokter|resep|prescription|lab|x-ray|rontgen|rawat|treatment|medical|medis|hospital|rumah sakit|klinik|clinic|apotek|pharmacy|terapi|therapy)\b/.test(lower)) return 'health';
    if (/\b(movie|film|bioskop|cinema|karaoke|game|bowling|spa|massage|pijat|gym|fitness|swimming|berenang|concert|konser|museum|park|taman|tiket|ticket)\b/.test(lower)) return 'entertainment';
    if (/\b(baju|shirt|celana|pants|sepatu|shoes|tas|bag|jam|watch|aksesori|accessories|elektronik|electronic|handphone|hp|laptop|komputer|computer|gadget|charger|kabel|cable|headset|earphone)\b/.test(lower)) return 'shopping';
    if (/\b(salon|haircut|potong rambut|manicure|pedicure|facial|waxing|eyelash|nail|kuku|creambath|blow|coloring|highlight|makeup)\b/.test(lower)) return 'beauty';
    return 'other_spending';
}

function isModifierLine(text) {
    const lower = text.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!lower) return true;
    if (getMoneyCandidates(text).length > 0) return false;
    if (/^(?:iced?|hot|large|medium|small|regular|tall|grande|venti|less\s*(?:sugar|sweet|ice)|normal\s*ice|no\s*sugar|cup|cone|bowl|glass|pint|mug|plate|slice|take\s*away|takeaway|dine\s*in)$/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/^(?:iced?|hot)\s+[-–]?\s*\d+\s*(?:oz|uz|ml)?$/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/^(?:\d+\s+)?(?:iced?|hot|tall|grande|venti|regular|large)\s*(?:rp|rm|sgd|\$)?\.?\s*[o0]\s*$/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/^(?:>|\+|\-|\*)\s*(?:iced|hot|less|normal|no|extra|sugar|ice|add|dairy|fresh|oat|soy)/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/(?:ice\s*level|sugar\s*level|dairy\s*milk|reguler\s*milk|tanpa\s*alat|request\s*-\s*less|have\s*here)/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/(?:rp|rm|sgd|\$)\.?\s*[o0]\s*$/i.test(lower)) return true;
    return false;
}

function isNonItemLine(text) {
    const lower = normLine(text).toLowerCase();
    if (!lower) return true;

    // Reject divider lines made of symbols
    if (/^[=\-*#~_.:\s]{3,}$/.test(lower) || /^[=\-*#]{2,}/.test(lower)) return true;

    // Company registration names / legal entities
    if (/\b(?:sdn\s*bhd|son\s*bhd|pte\s*ltd|pt\.\s*[a-z])/i.test(lower) || /\(\s*\d{6,}[A-Za-z\-]?\s*\)/.test(lower)) return true;

    // Service sizes (e.g. 16 oz, 8 uz)
    if (/\b\d+\s*(?:oz|uz|ml|cl|gr|gram)\b/i.test(lower)) return true;

    // Standalone modifier lines starting with symbols
    if (/^[-–+>]\s*/.test(lower)) return true;

    // Info, remarks, notes
    if (/^(?:info|purpose|remark|notes?|meja|table|guest|tamu)\s*[:.]/i.test(lower)) return true;

    // Customer name with seat/order codes (e.g., Dini Fito D3-502)
    if (/\bd\d{1,2}-\d{2,4}\b/i.test(lower)) return true;

    // Registration codes like No.CP: W10-2302-32000036
    if (/\b(?:no\.?cp|roc|reg\s*no|sst\s*id|sst\s*no|tax\s*id)\b/i.test(lower)) return true;

    for (const keyword of NON_ITEM_KEYWORDS) {
        if (keyword.length <= 4 || keyword === 'service' || keyword === 'server' || keyword === 'mode' || keyword === 'bill' || keyword === 'order' || keyword === 'tip' || keyword === 'pax' || keyword === 'tbl' || keyword === 'pos' || keyword === 'edc' || keyword === 'wa ' || keyword === 'line ') {
            const escaped = keyword.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&').replace(/\s+/g, '\\s+');
            if (new RegExp('(?:^|\\s)' + escaped + '(?:\\s|$|[:\\-,.])', 'i').test(lower)) return true;
        } else {
            if (lower.includes(keyword)) return true;
        }
    }

    if (/^[\d\s\/\-:.,]+$/.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?::\d{2})?\s*(?:am|pm)?\b/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/^\d+\s+items?$/i.test(lower)) return true;

    // Address & regional terms
    if (/(?:^|\b)(jl\.?|jln\b|jalan\b|j1\.?\b|street\b|rd\b|ave\b|blvd\b|no\.\s*\d|wilayah|persekutuan|kuala\s*lumpur|selangor|penang|johor|kab\.?|kabupaten|kec\.?|kecamatan|kel\.?|kelurahan|kota|denpasar|badung|gianyar|ubud|seminyak|kuta|canggu|sanur|singapore|bukit\s*bintang|lantai|lt\.?\s*\d|level\s*\d|rochor\s*canal|kalla\s*lumeut|peralatan)(?:\b|$)/i.test(lower)) return true;

    // Standalone postal codes ONLY when combined with address context
    if (/\b(?:80\d{3}|55\d{3}|18\d{4}|64\d{3})\b/.test(lower) && /(?:bali|denpasar|k\.l\.|klia|selangor|singapore|kuta|ubud|gianyar|badung)/i.test(lower)) return true;

    // Label lines: Queue No, Order ID, etc.
    if (/^(?:queue\s*no|quee\s*lo|q\s*no|order\s*id|receipt\s*number|order\s*ticket|no\.?\s*pesanan|pelanggan|no\.?\s*nota|kode\b|nomor\s*meja|no\.?\s*tlp|telp|no\.?\s*tip)\b/i.test(lower)) return true;

    // Standalone Dine In / Takeaway lines
    if (/^\*?\s*(?:mode|purpose|tipe\s*pesanan)?\s*[:.]?\s*\*?\s*(?:\d+\.)?\s*(?:dine\s*in|take\s*away|takeaway|t\/o|to\s*go)\s*\*?$/i.test(lower)) return true;

    // Modifiers without price
    if (isModifierLine(lower)) return true;

    // Discounts with parentheses: Grab Dine Out(15%) (4.950)
    if (/\bgrab\s*dine\s*out\b|\bdiscount\b|\bdiskon\b/i.test(lower) && /\([\d.,]+\)/.test(lower)) return true;

    if (/^[\d\s()+\-]{8,}$/.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/^\+?[\d\s()\-]{8,}$/.test(lower.replace(/^phone\s*:?\s*/i, ''))) return true;
    if (/^[-\s]*(rm|rp|idr|usd|aud|sgd|myr|\$|\u00a3|\u20ac|\u00a5)\s*$/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/^(?:rp|idr|sgd|usd|myr|rm)\.?\s*[o0]\s*$/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/^(?:rp|idr|sgd|usd|myr|rm)\.?\s*-[\d,.]+\s*$/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/@[\w]{2,}/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/^tlp[.\s]|^telp[.:\s]|^telepon[.:\s]|\bwa[.:\s]\d|\(wa\)/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/^no[.\s]?(bill|order|faktur|struk|receipt|nota|invoice)[:\s]/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/^(waiter|pelayan|waitress|kasir|cashier|server)[:\s]/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_\-]{9,}$/i.test(lower.trim()) && /\d/.test(lower) && /[a-z]/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/\b(?:my\s*card|visa|mastercard|amex|debit|credit)\s*\(\d{4}\)/i.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/\b(?:unit\s*price|item\s*name)\b/i.test(lower)) return true;

    return false;
}

function isUnitPriceLine(line) {
    const t = normLine(line);
    if (/^\d*\s*[xX]?\s*[@o0]/.test(t)) return true;
    if (/^@\s*(?:rp|idr|usd|aud|sgd|myr|rm|\$)?\.?\s*[\d,.]+$/i.test(t)) return true;
    return false;
}

function tryParseItemLine(line) {
    let cleaned = normLine(line).replace(/[\s\-=~*:]+$/, '').trim();
    if (isNonItemLine(cleaned)) return null;

    // Exclude negative amounts / discount lines
    if (/-\s*[\d,.]+/.test(cleaned) || /\([\d.,]+\)/.test(cleaned)) return null;

    let moneyCandidates = getMoneyCandidates(cleaned);
    if (!moneyCandidates.length) return null;

    let priceCandidate = moneyCandidates[moneyCandidates.length - 1];
    if (moneyCandidates.length >= 2 && priceCandidate.value < moneyCandidates[0].value * 0.20 && moneyCandidates[0].value > 0) {
        priceCandidate = moneyCandidates[0];
    }
    let rawName = cleaned.slice(0, moneyCandidates[0].start).trim();

    // Line with only numbers and unit price symbols has no item name
    const lettersOnly = rawName.replace(/[\d\s,./xX@o0\-]/g, '');
    if (lettersOnly.length === 0) return null;

    let quantity = 1;

    // Normalise 'xl' or 'xI' to 'x1'
    rawName = rawName.replace(/\b[xX][lI1]\b/g, 'x1');

    const leadingQty = rawName.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s+([A-Za-z*].+)$/);
    if (leadingQty) {
        const candidateQty = parseInt(leadingQty[1], 10);
        const candidateName = leadingQty[2].trim();
        if (!isNonItemLine(candidateName) && candidateName.length >= 2 && candidateQty >= 1 && candidateQty <= 99) {
            rawName = candidateName;
            quantity = candidateQty;
        }
    }

    if (quantity === 1) {
        const midQtyX = rawName.match(/^(.+?)\s+[xX](\d{1,2})\b(.*)$/);
        if (midQtyX) {
            const candidateName = (midQtyX[1] + ' ' + (midQtyX[3] || '')).trim();
            const candidateQty = parseInt(midQtyX[2], 10);
            if (!isNonItemLine(candidateName) && candidateName.length >= 2 && candidateQty > 0) {
                rawName = candidateName;
                quantity = candidateQty;
            }
        }
    }

    if (quantity === 1) {
        const trailingQty = rawName.match(/^(.+?)\s+(\d{1,2})\s*$/);
        if (trailingQty) {
            const candidateName = trailingQty[1].trim();
            const candidateQty = parseInt(trailingQty[2], 10);
            if (!isNonItemLine(candidateName) && candidateName.length >= 2 && candidateQty >= 1 && candidateQty <= 99) {
                rawName = candidateName;
                quantity = candidateQty;
            }
        }
    }

    const name = rawName
        .replace(/^\*+\s*/, '')
        .replace(/\s*(?:\((?:dine\s*in|take\s*away|takeaway|t\/o)\)|dine\s*in|take\s*away|takeaway|t\/o)\s*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s*(?:rp|rm|sgd|usd|myr|\$)\.?\s*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s*[xX@]\s*$/, '')
        .trim();

    if (!name || name.length < 2 || isNonItemLine(name)) return null;
    if (/^[-\s]*(rm|rp|idr|usd|aud|sgd|myr|\$|\u00a3|\u20ac|\u00a5)\s*$/i.test(name)) return null;
    if (priceCandidate.value <= 0) return null;

    return { name, quantity, price: priceCandidate.value };
}

function tryParseMultiLineItem(lines, index) {
    const nameLine = lines[index];
    if (!nameLine) return null;

    // Pattern E: Quantity + Price line followed by Item Name line
    if (index + 1 < lines.length) {
        const nextLineCandidate = lines[index + 1];
        const qtyPriceMatch = nameLine.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s+(?:[A-Za-z0-9*#%&@\s-]{1,8}\s+)?([\d.,]+)\s*$/);
        if (qtyPriceMatch && !isNonItemLine(nextLineCandidate) && getMoneyCandidates(nextLineCandidate).length === 0 && nextLineCandidate.length >= 3) {
            const qty = parseInt(qtyPriceMatch[1], 10);
            const val = parseMoney(qtyPriceMatch[2]);
            if (qty >= 1 && qty <= 99 && val != null && val > 0) {
                return { name: nextLineCandidate.trim(), quantity: qty, price: val, consumedNext: 1 };
            }
        }
    }

    if (getMoneyCandidates(nameLine).length > 0) return null;
    if (isNonItemLine(nameLine)) return null;
    if (isUnitPriceLine(nameLine)) return null;
    if (nameLine.length < 2) return null;

    let cleanName = nameLine.replace(/^\*+\s*/, '').trim();
    let leadingQty = 1;

    const leadingQtyMatch = cleanName.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s+([A-Za-z*].+)$/);
    if (leadingQtyMatch && parseInt(leadingQtyMatch[1], 10) >= 1 && parseInt(leadingQtyMatch[1], 10) <= 99) {
        leadingQty = parseInt(leadingQtyMatch[1], 10);
        cleanName = leadingQtyMatch[2].trim();
    }

    cleanName = cleanName
        .replace(/\s*(?:\((?:dine\s*in|take\s*away|takeaway|t\/o)\)|dine\s*in|take\s*away|takeaway|t\/o)\s*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s*(?:rp|rm|sgd|usd|myr|\$)\.?\s*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s*[xX@]\s*$/, '')
        .trim();

    if (!cleanName || cleanName.length < 2 || isNonItemLine(cleanName)) return null;

    let targetOffset = 1;
    let nextLine = lines[index + targetOffset];
    if (nextLine && isModifierLine(nextLine) && index + targetOffset + 1 < lines.length) {
        targetOffset = 2;
        nextLine = lines[index + targetOffset];
    }
    if (!nextLine) return null;

    const nextLower = nextLine.toLowerCase();
    if (/(subtotal|sub total|subttl|total|tax|pajak|ppn|service|grand)/i.test(nextLower)) return null;

    // Pattern A: nextLine is "5x @40.000 200.000" or "2x @60.000 120.000" or "1x @33.000 33.000"
    const qtyAtUnitSameLine = nextLine.match(/^(\d+)\s*[xX]\s*[@o0]?\s*(?:rp|idr|usd|myr|rm|\$)?\.?\s*([\d,.]+)/i);
    if (qtyAtUnitSameLine) {
        const qty = parseInt(qtyAtUnitSameLine[1], 10) || leadingQty;
        const nextCandidatesA = getMoneyCandidates(nextLine);
        if (nextCandidatesA.length >= 2) {
            const lineTotal = nextCandidatesA[nextCandidatesA.length - 1].value;
            if (lineTotal > 0) {
                return { name: cleanName, quantity: qty, price: lineTotal, consumedNext: targetOffset };
            }
        }
        if (nextCandidatesA.length === 1) {
            const val = nextCandidatesA[0].value;
            const thirdLine = lines[index + targetOffset + 1];
            if (thirdLine && !/(subtotal|sub total|subttl|total|tax|pajak|ppn|service|grand)/i.test(thirdLine.toLowerCase())) {
                const thirdCandidatesA = getMoneyCandidates(thirdLine);
                if (thirdCandidatesA.length === 1 && !tryParseItemLine(thirdLine)) {
