================ FILE: modules/ReceiptOCR/android/src/main/java/expo/modules/receiptocr/ReceiptOCRModule.kt ================

package expo.modules.receiptocr

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Matrix
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.net.Uri
import com.google.mlkit.vision.common.InputImage
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.TextRecognition
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.latin.TextRecognizerOptions
import expo.modules.kotlin.Promise
import expo.modules.kotlin.modules.Module
import expo.modules.kotlin.modules.ModuleDefinition
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.util.UUID
import kotlin.math.max
import kotlin.math.min
import kotlin.math.sqrt

class ReceiptOCRModule : Module() {
  override fun definition() = ModuleDefinition {
    Name("ReceiptOCRModule")

    // ─── recognizeText ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    AsyncFunction("recognizeText") { imageUri: String, promise: Promise ->
      try {
        val cleanUri = imageUri.replace("file://", "")
        val file = File(cleanUri)
        if (!file.exists()) {
          promise.reject("ERR_IMAGE", "File does not exist: $imageUri", null)
          return@AsyncFunction
        }
        val options = BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inJustDecodeBounds = true }
        BitmapFactory.decodeFile(cleanUri, options)
        val imgWidth = max(1, options.outWidth)
        val imgHeight = max(1, options.outHeight)

        val image = InputImage.fromFilePath(context, Uri.fromFile(file))
        val recognizer = TextRecognition.getClient(TextRecognizerOptions.DEFAULT_OPTIONS)

        recognizer.process(image)
          .addOnSuccessListener { visionText ->
            val blocks = mutableListOf<Map<String, Any?>>()
            for (block in visionText.textBlocks) {
              for (line in block.lines) {
                val box = line.boundingBox
                val bbox = if (box != null) {
                  mapOf(
                    "x" to box.left.toDouble() / imgWidth,
                    "y" to box.top.toDouble() / imgHeight,
                    "w" to box.width().toDouble() / imgWidth,
                    "h" to box.height().toDouble() / imgHeight
                  )
                } else {
                  mapOf("x" to 0.0, "y" to 0.0, "w" to 0.0, "h" to 0.0)
                }
                blocks.add(mapOf(
                  "text" to line.text,
                  "confidence" to 1.0,
                  "bbox" to bbox
                ))
              }
            }
            promise.resolve(mapOf("blocks" to blocks))
          }
          .addOnFailureListener { e ->
            promise.reject("ERR_OCR", e.localizedMessage, e)
          }
      } catch (e: Exception) {
        promise.reject("ERR_OCR", e.localizedMessage, e)
      }
    }

    // ─── detectRectangle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    AsyncFunction("detectRectangle") { imageUri: String, promise: Promise ->
      try {
        val cleanUri = imageUri.replace("file://", "")
        val file = File(cleanUri)
        if (!file.exists()) {
          promise.reject("ERR_IMAGE", "File does not exist: $imageUri", null)
          return@AsyncFunction
        }
        val dimOpts = BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inJustDecodeBounds = true }
        BitmapFactory.decodeFile(cleanUri, dimOpts)
        val imgWidth = max(1, dimOpts.outWidth)
        val imgHeight = max(1, dimOpts.outHeight)

        val image = InputImage.fromFilePath(context, Uri.fromFile(file))
        val recognizer = TextRecognition.getClient(TextRecognizerOptions.DEFAULT_OPTIONS)

        recognizer.process(image)
          .addOnSuccessListener { visionText ->
            data class NormBox(val l: Double, val t: Double, val r: Double, val b: Double)
            val boxes = mutableListOf<NormBox>()

            for (block in visionText.textBlocks) {
              val box = block.boundingBox ?: continue
              val bw = box.width().toDouble() / imgWidth
              val bh = box.height().toDouble() / imgHeight
              if (bw < 0.003 || bh < 0.003) continue
              boxes.add(NormBox(
                box.left.toDouble() / imgWidth,
                box.top.toDouble() / imgHeight,
                box.right.toDouble() / imgWidth,
                box.bottom.toDouble() / imgHeight
              ))
            }

            if (boxes.isEmpty()) {
              promise.resolve(null)
              return@addOnSuccessListener
            }

            val lefts   = boxes.map { it.l }.sorted()
            val tops    = boxes.map { it.t }.sorted()
            val rights  = boxes.map { it.r }.sorted()
            val bottoms = boxes.map { it.b }.sorted()

            fun <T : Comparable<T>> List<T>.pct(p: Double): T =
              this[(size * p).toInt().coerceIn(0, size - 1)]

            val rawMinX = lefts.pct(0.05)
            val rawMaxX = rights.pct(0.95)
            val rawMinY = tops.pct(0.03)
            val rawMaxY = bottoms.pct(0.97)

            if (rawMinX >= rawMaxX || rawMinY >= rawMaxY) {
              promise.resolve(null)
              return@addOnSuccessListener
            }

            // Asymmetric paper margin: 6% horizontal, 8% vertical
            val padX = 0.06
            val padY = 0.08
            val minX = max(0.01, rawMinX - padX)
            val minY = max(0.01, rawMinY - padY)
            val maxX = min(0.99, rawMaxX + padX)
            val maxY = min(0.99, rawMaxY + padY)

            promise.resolve(mapOf(
              "topLeft"     to mapOf("x" to minX, "y" to minY),
              "topRight"    to mapOf("x" to maxX, "y" to minY),
              "bottomRight" to mapOf("x" to maxX, "y" to maxY),
              "bottomLeft"  to mapOf("x" to minX, "y" to maxY)
            ))
          }
          .addOnFailureListener { e ->
            promise.reject("ERR_DETECT", e.localizedMessage, e)
          }
      } catch (e: Exception) {
        promise.reject("ERR_DETECT", e.localizedMessage, e)
      }
    }

    // ─── perspectiveCorrect ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // True 4-point perspective warp using android.graphics.Matrix.setPolyToPoly.
    // Maps the user-defined quadrilateral to a flat, axis-aligned rectangle.
    AsyncFunction("perspectiveCorrect") { imageUri: String, corners: Map<String, Map<String, Double>>, promise: Promise ->
      try {
        val cleanUri = imageUri.replace("file://", "")
        val file = File(cleanUri)
        if (!file.exists()) {
          promise.reject("ERR_IMAGE", "File does not exist: $imageUri", null)
          return@AsyncFunction
        }

        // Decode full bitmap (handles EXIF via InputImage on device but BitmapFactory is fine
        // here since we've already EXIF-normalised the URI in JS before calling this)
        val srcBitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeFile(cleanUri)
          ?: run { promise.reject("ERR_IMAGE", "Failed to decode bitmap", null); return@AsyncFunction }

        val imgW = srcBitmap.width.toFloat()
        val imgH = srcBitmap.height.toFloat()

        val tl = corners["topLeft"]     ?: emptyMap()
        val tr = corners["topRight"]    ?: emptyMap()
        val br = corners["bottomRight"] ?: emptyMap()
        val bl = corners["bottomLeft"]  ?: emptyMap()

        fun cx(m: Map<String, Double>) = ((m["x"] ?: 0.0) * imgW).toFloat()
        fun cy(m: Map<String, Double>) = ((m["y"] ?: 0.0) * imgH).toFloat()
        fun dist(ax: Float, ay: Float, bx: Float, by: Float) =
          sqrt(((bx - ax) * (bx - ax) + (by - ay) * (by - ay)).toDouble()).toFloat()

        val tlX = cx(tl); val tlY = cy(tl)
        val trX = cx(tr); val trY = cy(tr)
        val brX = cx(br); val brY = cy(br)
        val blX = cx(bl); val blY = cy(bl)

        // Output size: use the lengths of the actual quad edges so the receipt fills
        // the output without black bars from aspect mismatch
        val topW  = dist(tlX, tlY, trX, trY)
        val botW  = dist(blX, blY, brX, brY)
        val leftH = dist(tlX, tlY, blX, blY)
        val rightH = dist(trX, trY, brX, brY)

        val outW = max(topW, botW).toInt().coerceAtLeast(1)
        val outH = max(leftH, rightH).toInt().coerceAtLeast(1)

        // Source quad → destination rectangle (perspective warp)
        val srcPts = floatArrayOf(tlX, tlY, trX, trY, brX, brY, blX, blY)
        val dstPts = floatArrayOf(
          0f, 0f,
          outW.toFloat(), 0f,
          outW.toFloat(), outH.toFloat(),
          0f, outH.toFloat()
        )

        val matrix = Matrix()
        val ok = matrix.setPolyToPoly(srcPts, 0, dstPts, 0, 4)
        if (!ok) {
          srcBitmap.recycle()
          promise.reject("ERR_WARP", "setPolyToPoly failed — degenerate quad", null)
          return@AsyncFunction
        }

        val result = Bitmap.createBitmap(outW, outH, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        val canvas = Canvas(result)
        canvas.drawBitmap(srcBitmap, matrix, Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG))
        srcBitmap.recycle()

        val tempFile = File(context.cacheDir, "warp_${UUID.randomUUID()}.jpg")
        FileOutputStream(tempFile).use { out ->
          result.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 95, out)
        }
        result.recycle()

        promise.resolve(mapOf(
          "uri"    to "file://${tempFile.absolutePath}",
          "width"  to outW,
          "height" to outH
        ))
      } catch (e: Exception) {
        promise.reject("ERR_WARP", e.localizedMessage, e)
      }
    }
  }

  private val context: android.content.Context
    get() = appContext.reactContext ?: throw IllegalStateException("ReactContext is null")
}


================ FILE: ios/Podfile ================

require File.join(File.dirname(`node --print "require.resolve('expo/package.json')"`), "scripts/autolinking")
require File.join(File.dirname(`node --print "require.resolve('react-native/package.json')"`), "scripts/react_native_pods")

require 'json'
podfile_properties = JSON.parse(File.read(File.join(__dir__, 'Podfile.properties.json'))) rescue {}

ENV['RCT_NEW_ARCH_ENABLED'] = podfile_properties['newArchEnabled'] == 'true' ? '1' : '0'
ENV['EX_DEV_CLIENT_NETWORK_INSPECTOR'] = podfile_properties['EX_DEV_CLIENT_NETWORK_INSPECTOR']

platform :ios, podfile_properties['ios.deploymentTarget'] || '15.1'
install! 'cocoapods',
  :deterministic_uuids => false

prepare_react_native_project!

target 'FinanceFlow' do
  use_expo_modules!
  pod 'ReceiptOCRModule', :path => '../modules/ReceiptOCR/ios'

  if ENV['EXPO_USE_COMMUNITY_AUTOLINKING'] == '1'
    config_command = ['node', '-e', "process.argv=['', '', 'config'];require('@react-native-community/cli').run()"];
  else
    config_command = [
      'node',
      '--no-warnings',
      '--eval',
      'require(require.resolve(\'expo-modules-autolinking\', { paths: [require.resolve(\'expo/package.json\')] }))(process.argv.slice(1))',
      'react-native-config',
      '--json',
      '--platform',
      'ios'
    ]
  end

  config = use_native_modules!(config_command)

  use_frameworks! :linkage => podfile_properties['ios.useFrameworks'].to_sym if podfile_properties['ios.useFrameworks']
  use_frameworks! :linkage => ENV['USE_FRAMEWORKS'].to_sym if ENV['USE_FRAMEWORKS']

  use_react_native!(
    :path => config[:reactNativePath],
    :hermes_enabled => podfile_properties['expo.jsEngine'] == nil || podfile_properties['expo.jsEngine'] == 'hermes',
    # An absolute path to your application root.
    :app_path => "#{Pod::Config.instance.installation_root}/..",
    :privacy_file_aggregation_enabled => podfile_properties['apple.privacyManifestAggregationEnabled'] != 'false',
  )

  post_install do |installer|
    react_native_post_install(
      installer,
      config[:reactNativePath],
      :mac_catalyst_enabled => false,
      :ccache_enabled => podfile_properties['apple.ccacheEnabled'] == 'true',
    )

    # This is necessary for Xcode 14, because it signs resource bundles by default
    # when building for devices.
    installer.target_installation_results.pod_target_installation_results
      .each do |pod_name, target_installation_result|
      target_installation_result.resource_bundle_targets.each do |resource_bundle_target|
        resource_bundle_target.build_configurations.each do |config|
          config.build_settings['CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED'] = 'NO'
        end
      end
    end
    # LLAMA_RN_CXX20: Force C++20 on all Pods
    installer.pods_project.targets.each do |target|
      target.build_configurations.each do |config|
        config.build_settings['CLANG_CXX_LANGUAGE_STANDARD'] = 'gnu++20'
        config.build_settings['CLANG_CXX_LIBRARY'] = 'libc++'
        config.build_settings['OTHER_CPLUSPLUSFLAGS'] = '$(inherited) -std=gnu++20'
      end
    end

    # FIX: fmt 11.x + Apple Clang 21 + C++20 consteval regression
    # Apple Clang 21 (Xcode 16.4) introduced a consteval regression that breaks fmt 11.
    # We patch fmt/base.h to disable consteval for Apple Clang 21.x builds.
    fmt_base_h = File.join(__dir__, 'Pods/fmt/include/fmt/base.h')
    if File.exist?(fmt_base_h)
      content = File.read(fmt_base_h)
      old_str = '#elif defined(__apple_build_version__) && __apple_build_version__ < 14000029L'
      new_str = '#elif defined(__apple_build_version__) && (__apple_build_version__ < 14000029L || __apple_build_version__ >= 21000000L)'
      if content.include?(old_str)
        File.write(fmt_base_h, content.gsub(old_str, new_str))
        puts 'Patched fmt/base.h for Apple Clang 21 consteval fix'
      end
    end

    # FIX: Fabric RCTSurfaceTouchHandler Inconsistency between local and UIKit touch registries assertion crash
    touch_handler_mm = File.join(__dir__, '../node_modules/react-native/React/Fabric/RCTSurfaceTouchHandler.mm')
    if File.exist?(touch_handler_mm)
      content = File.read(touch_handler_mm)
      target_assertion = 'RCTAssert(iterator != _activeTouches.end(), @"Inconsistency between local and UIKit touch registries");'
      if content.include?(target_assertion)
        File.write(touch_handler_mm, content.gsub(target_assertion, '// RCTAssert bypassed: guarded by if (iterator == _activeTouches.end()) continue; below'))
        puts 'Patched RCTSurfaceTouchHandler.mm to prevent touch registry assertion crash'
      end
    end

  end
end

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
<plist version="1.0">

================ FILE: ios/FinanceFlow/WidgetDataModule.swift ================

//
//  WidgetDataModule.swift
//  FinanceFlow
//
//  Native module that receives widget data from React Native
//  and writes it to the shared App Group UserDefaults so WidgetKit
//  widgets can read it.
//

import Foundation
import WidgetKit

@objc(WidgetDataModule)
class WidgetDataModule: NSObject {

    let APP_GROUP = "group.com.financeflow.app"
    let WIDGET_DATA_KEY = "financeflow_widget_data"

    @objc
    func updateWidgetData(_ json: String,
                          resolver resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock,
                          rejecter reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock) {
        if let defaults = UserDefaults(suiteName: APP_GROUP) {
            defaults.set(json, forKey: WIDGET_DATA_KEY)
            defaults.synchronize()
            // Tell WidgetKit to reload all timelines
            if #available(iOS 14.0, *) {
                WidgetCenter.shared.reloadAllTimelines()
            }
            resolve(nil)
        } else {
            reject("WIDGET_ERROR", "Could not access App Group UserDefaults", nil)
        }
    }

    @objc
    static func requiresMainQueueSetup() -> Bool { false }
}

================ FILE: ios/FinanceFlowWidget/FinanceFlowWidget.swift ================

//
//  FinanceFlowWidget.swift
//  FinanceFlowWidget
//
//  WidgetKit extension providing two widgets:
//  1. Quick Actions Widget — log expense, scan receipt, add savings
//  2. Daily Allowance Widget — glanceable budget remaining
//

import WidgetKit
import SwiftUI

// ─── Shared Data Model ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

let APP_GROUP = "group.com.financeflow.app"
let WIDGET_DATA_KEY = "financeflow_widget_data"

struct DailyAllowanceData {
    var dailyBudget: Double = 0
    var todaySpent: Double = 0
    var remaining: Double = 0
    var percentage: Int = 0
    var currency: String = "USD"
}

struct SavingsGoalData {
    var name: String = ""
    var savedAmount: Double = 0
    var targetAmount: Double = 0
    var percentage: Int = 0
}

struct WidgetData {
    var allowance: DailyAllowanceData = DailyAllowanceData()
    var nearestGoal: SavingsGoalData? = nil
    var hasGoals: Bool = false
    var updatedAt: Date = Date()
}

func loadWidgetData() -> WidgetData {
    guard
        let defaults = UserDefaults(suiteName: APP_GROUP),
        let json = defaults.string(forKey: WIDGET_DATA_KEY),
        let data = json.data(using: .utf8),
        let obj = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any]
    else {
        return WidgetData()
    }

    var wd = WidgetData()
    wd.hasGoals = obj["hasGoals"] as? Bool ?? false
    wd.updatedAt = Date()

    if let allowanceObj = obj["dailyAllowance"] as? [String: Any] {
        wd.allowance.dailyBudget = allowanceObj["dailyBudget"] as? Double ?? 0
        wd.allowance.todaySpent = allowanceObj["todaySpent"] as? Double ?? 0
        wd.allowance.remaining = allowanceObj["remaining"] as? Double ?? 0
        wd.allowance.percentage = allowanceObj["percentage"] as? Int ?? 0
        wd.allowance.currency = obj["currency"] as? String ?? "USD"
    }

    if let savingsObj = obj["savings"] as? [String: Any],
       let goalObj = savingsObj["nearestGoal"] as? [String: Any] {
        var goal = SavingsGoalData()
        goal.name = goalObj["name"] as? String ?? "Goal"
        goal.savedAmount = goalObj["savedAmount"] as? Double ?? 0
        goal.targetAmount = goalObj["targetAmount"] as? Double ?? 0
        goal.percentage = goalObj["percentage"] as? Int ?? 0
        wd.nearestGoal = goal
    }

    return wd
}

func formatAmount(_ amount: Double, currency: String) -> String {
    let symbol: String
    switch currency {
    case "USD": symbol = "$"
    case "IDR": symbol = "Rp "
    case "EUR": symbol = "€"
    case "GBP": symbol = "£"
    case "JPY": symbol = "¥"
    case "MYR": symbol = "RM "
    case "SGD": symbol = "S$"
    case "AUD": symbol = "A$"
    case "CAD": symbol = "C$"
    default: symbol = currency + " "
    }
    let absVal = Swift.abs(amount)
    let prefix = amount < 0 ? "-" : ""
    let formatter = NumberFormatter()
    formatter.numberStyle = .decimal
    formatter.maximumFractionDigits = (absVal >= 1000 || ["IDR", "JPY", "KRW", "VND"].contains(currency)) ? 0 : 2
    formatter.minimumFractionDigits = 0
    let numStr = formatter.string(from: NSNumber(value: absVal)) ?? String(format: "%.0f", absVal)
    return "\(prefix)\(symbol)\(numStr)"
}

// ─── Quick Actions Widget ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

struct QuickActionsEntry: TimelineEntry {
    let date: Date
}

struct QuickActionsProvider: TimelineProvider {
    func placeholder(in context: Context) -> QuickActionsEntry {
        QuickActionsEntry(date: Date())
    }
    func getSnapshot(in context: Context, completion: @escaping (QuickActionsEntry) -> Void) {
        completion(QuickActionsEntry(date: Date()))
    }
    func getTimeline(in context: Context, completion: @escaping (Timeline<QuickActionsEntry>) -> Void) {
        let entry = QuickActionsEntry(date: Date())
        let next = Calendar.current.date(byAdding: .hour, value: 1, to: Date())!
        completion(Timeline(entries: [entry], policy: .after(next)))
    }
}

struct QuickActionsWidgetView: View {
    var body: some View {
        ZStack {
            LinearGradient(
                colors: [Color(red: 0.05, green: 0.07, blue: 0.12), Color(red: 0.08, green: 0.05, blue: 0.15)],
                startPoint: .topLeading,
                endPoint: .bottomTrailing
            )

            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
                // Header
                HStack(spacing: 5) {
                    Image(systemName: "bolt.fill")
                        .foregroundColor(Color(red: 1, green: 0.4, blue: 0))
                        .font(.system(size: 10, weight: .bold))
                    Text("FinanceFlow")
                        .font(.system(size: 10, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(.white.opacity(0.6))
                    Spacer()
                }
                .padding(.bottom, 4)

                // Three action buttons
                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                    ActionButton(
                        systemImage: "plus.circle.fill",
                        label: "Expense",
                        url: "financeflow://add-transaction",
                        color: Color(red: 1, green: 0.4, blue: 0)
                    )
                    ActionButton(
                        systemImage: "camera.fill",
                        label: "Receipt",
                        url: "financeflow://receipt-scanner",
                        color: Color(red: 0.13, green: 0.7, blue: 0.53)
                    )
                    ActionButton(
                        systemImage: "arrow.up.circle.fill",
                        label: "Savings",
                        url: "financeflow://savings?action=add",
                        color: Color(red: 0.36, green: 0.45, blue: 0.9)
                    )
                }
            }
            .padding(14)
        }
    }
}

struct ActionButton: View {
    var systemImage: String
    var label: String
    var url: String
    var color: Color

    var body: some View {
        Link(destination: URL(string: url)!) {
            VStack(spacing: 5) {
                ZStack {
                    RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10)
                        .fill(color.opacity(0.15))
                        .frame(width: 42, height: 42)
                    Image(systemName: systemImage)
                        .foregroundColor(color)
                        .font(.system(size: 18, weight: .semibold))
                }
                Text(label)
                    .font(.system(size: 9, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(.white.opacity(0.75))
            }
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
        }
    }
}

struct QuickActionsWidget: Widget {
    let kind = "FinanceFlowQuickActions"
    var body: some WidgetConfiguration {
        StaticConfiguration(kind: kind, provider: QuickActionsProvider()) { _ in
            if #available(iOS 17.0, *) {
                QuickActionsWidgetView()
                    .containerBackground(for: .widget) {
                        Color(red: 0.05, green: 0.07, blue: 0.12)
                    }
            } else {
                QuickActionsWidgetView()
            }
        }
        .configurationDisplayName("Quick Actions")
        .description("One-tap log expense, scan receipt, or add savings.")
        .supportedFamilies([.systemSmall, .systemMedium])
    }
}

// ─── Daily Allowance Widget ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

struct AllowanceEntry: TimelineEntry {
    let date: Date
    let data: WidgetData
}

struct AllowanceProvider: TimelineProvider {
    func placeholder(in context: Context) -> AllowanceEntry {
        AllowanceEntry(date: Date(), data: WidgetData())
    }
    func getSnapshot(in context: Context, completion: @escaping (AllowanceEntry) -> Void) {
        completion(AllowanceEntry(date: Date(), data: loadWidgetData()))
    }
    func getTimeline(in context: Context, completion: @escaping (Timeline<AllowanceEntry>) -> Void) {
        let data = loadWidgetData()
        let entry = AllowanceEntry(date: Date(), data: data)
        let next = Calendar.current.date(byAdding: .minute, value: 30, to: Date())!
        completion(Timeline(entries: [entry], policy: .after(next)))
    }
}

struct DailyAllowanceWidgetView: View {
    var entry: AllowanceEntry

    var allowance: DailyAllowanceData { entry.data.allowance }
    var nearestGoal: SavingsGoalData? { entry.data.nearestGoal }

    var barColor: Color {
        switch allowance.percentage {
        case ..<70:  return Color(red: 0.13, green: 0.7, blue: 0.38)
        case ..<100: return Color(red: 1, green: 0.4, blue: 0)
        default:     return Color(red: 0.93, green: 0.27, blue: 0.27)
        }
    }

    var body: some View {
        Link(destination: URL(string: "financeflow://")!) {
            ZStack {
                LinearGradient(
                    colors: [Color(red: 0.05, green: 0.07, blue: 0.12), Color(red: 0.08, green: 0.05, blue: 0.15)],
                    startPoint: .topLeading,
                    endPoint: .bottomTrailing
                )

                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                    // Header
                    HStack(spacing: 4) {
                        Image(systemName: "chart.bar.fill")
                            .foregroundColor(Color(red: 1, green: 0.4, blue: 0))
                            .font(.system(size: 9, weight: .bold))
                        Text("Today's Budget")
                            .font(.system(size: 9, weight: .medium))
                            .foregroundColor(.white.opacity(0.55))
                        Spacer()
                    }

                    Spacer()

                    // Remaining amount
                    Text(formatAmount(allowance.remaining, currency: allowance.currency))
                        .font(.system(size: 26, weight: .black, design: .rounded))
                        .foregroundColor(barColor)
                        .minimumScaleFactor(0.6)
                        .lineLimit(1)

                    Text(allowance.remaining >= 0 ? "remaining today" : "over budget")
                        .font(.system(size: 9, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(.white.opacity(0.5))
                        .padding(.bottom, 4)

                    // Progress bar
                    GeometryReader { geo in
                        ZStack(alignment: .leading) {
                            RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 3)
                                .fill(.white.opacity(0.12))
                                .frame(height: 5)
                            RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 3)
                                .fill(barColor)
                                .frame(width: geo.size.width * CGFloat(min(allowance.percentage, 100)) / 100.0, height: 5)
                        }
                    }
                    .frame(height: 5)
                    .padding(.bottom, 3)

                    // Spent / Budget row
                    HStack {
                        Text("Spent: \(formatAmount(allowance.todaySpent, currency: allowance.currency))")
                            .font(.system(size: 8, weight: .regular))
                            .foregroundColor(.white.opacity(0.45))
                        Spacer()
                        Text("Budget: \(formatAmount(allowance.dailyBudget, currency: allowance.currency))")
                            .font(.system(size: 8, weight: .regular))
                            .foregroundColor(.white.opacity(0.45))
                    }

                    // Nearest savings goal reminder
                    if let goal = nearestGoal {
                        Divider().background(.white.opacity(0.1)).padding(.vertical, 3)
                        HStack(spacing: 4) {
                            Image(systemName: "arrow.up.circle.fill")
                                .foregroundColor(Color(red: 0.36, green: 0.45, blue: 0.9))
                                .font(.system(size: 9))
                            Text("\(goal.name): \(goal.percentage)%")
                                .font(.system(size: 9, weight: .medium))
                                .foregroundColor(.white.opacity(0.65))
                                .lineLimit(1)
                            Spacer()
                            Text("Add savings →")
                                .font(.system(size: 8, weight: .medium))
                                .foregroundColor(Color(red: 0.36, green: 0.45, blue: 0.9))
                        }
                    }
                }
                .padding(12)
            }
        }
    }
}

struct DailyAllowanceWidget: Widget {
    let kind = "FinanceFlowDailyAllowance"
    var body: some WidgetConfiguration {
        StaticConfiguration(kind: kind, provider: AllowanceProvider()) { entry in
            if #available(iOS 17.0, *) {
                DailyAllowanceWidgetView(entry: entry)
                    .containerBackground(for: .widget) {
                        Color(red: 0.05, green: 0.07, blue: 0.12)
                    }
            } else {
                DailyAllowanceWidgetView(entry: entry)
            }
        }
        .configurationDisplayName("Daily Allowance")
        .description("Today's remaining budget at a glance.")
        .supportedFamilies([.systemSmall, .systemMedium])
    }
}

// ─── Widget Bundle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@main
struct FinanceFlowWidgetBundle: WidgetBundle {
    var body: some Widget {
        QuickActionsWidget()
        DailyAllowanceWidget()
    }
}
